# Player Narrative Extraction: Acts 3-4 (Chapters 41-136)

Extracted from Brigit's player notes (with occasional guest perspectives).
Source: Mardov DnD Reference Guide

**Coverage**: 63 files covering 61 unique chapter numbers out of 96 possible (41-136).
**Missing chapters** (35): 41-48, 50-63, 65-68, 70-71, 73, 80-81, 89, 122, 129, 134.
Most gaps are in the 41-63 range (early Act 3), suggesting Brigit's notes started mid-act.
Chapter 88/89 is combined into one file. Chapter 112 has two separate note files. Chapter 115 is split into A (Rufus) and B (Brigit).

**Note**: Chapters not present in the source files are gaps in the player notes.
Chapters with T suffix = Tieflings track, D suffix = Dwarves track, no suffix = Full Party.

### Authors
- **Brigit**: Primary note-taker (57 chapters)
- **Borgot**: Chapter 125
- **Rufus**: Chapter 115A
- **Randall**: Chapter 76T
- **Group**: Chapter 111
- **Fenrir**: Chapter 92T

## Chapter 49T -- No running by the pool
**Author**: Brigit
**Party Track**: Tieflings

### What Happened

TLDR last time TEAM BIRD At the bank, there's lots of giants acting as guards for someone carrying a heavy pack on his shoulder - something like a marshmallow. (packs of 5) And the main people they're seeing are Gin - Genie's How does the bank work? People are walking in, dumping their gold on the floor and after some time it disappears and Electrum appears There's a genie walking around in beggars clothes asking to spare a coin And there seems to be a teller? There's someone hanging outside that looks like a teller, but he's outside. They head inside the bank It's kind of like marble, but covered in clear blue. And inside the bank is perfectly spherical and there's 12 genasi that are creating a magic circle. Every hour one of the genasi is struck by lightning and they're turned electric blue, and the lightning shoots out, hits two of the barrels we saw carried in earlier, and turns into electrum Sprocket and Stalwart walk up, Stalwart attempts to be regal and acts like Sprocket is wealthy. The standard rate applies - 2:1 But they don't trade electrum for gold, there's no reason to leave Cloud City It actually makes sense that it's 2:1, the two barrels of cloud are combined with the lightning to form the electrum. Sprocket spares two gold coins for the beggar genie. Why did you give me this coin? To improve your life. Good answer! A BRONZE ANCIENT DRAGON APPEARS Don't worry, your secret is safe with me. AND GIVES 300 ELECTRUM Zephyron - works at the bank apparently. "Do you think he knows Tiberious?" Stalwart places some gold on the floor, each individual piece is grabbed by unseen servants, and taken into the magic circle, and then the unseen servants bring back electrum. TEAM DIPLOMAT Randall is on stage, and half his pan flute isn't working. Insight - Randall doesn't seem to mind and he's very distracted and watching the crowd intently. He points us to the concierge desk. The crowd - there's a flock of Geese and there's a burly Crane. They're forming a wall holding back the crowd. And there's a super tall puffin, and doesn't seem to be a puffin - his glass is going into his beak. To the concierge desk! The guest services behind the concierge desk - is also the basement where the fiends are. Also, he wants us to change rooms - maybe we could get something on the first floor The noble - Matthia Nunn, is traveling from Windhaven tomorrow and will need the presidential suite last night. Minnie made the Hero's Feast last night - but it wasn't poisoned We should probably save Randall. How long has he been playing? Randall wants to earn his room first - but if we can get him to stop, Mr. Tux will give him the room for free. To create a distraction! Fen is going to try and start a fight. Brigit is going to head to the kitchen door and try to get Randall to come this way once he can. Fen is going to pretend to be a drunk and grab the geese in the front - the Flying V formation. The geese are not happy. Fen gets grabbed and is grappled by the big burly crane. Gets thrown to the ground Brigit sees this isn't going well, Brigit summons 8 Boars and sends them charging through the crowd - helped by Randall who plays loudly to distract the magic. THE CROWD FREAKS, and flies! Randall tries to make his way to us and fails. The crane pulls down Fen and tells him to run. Brigit goes to grab Randall, succeeds The crane works to get towards Randall and Fen tries to distract him, but it doesn't work. Brigit and Randall book it to the kitchen. Brigit, Randall, and Varis make it into the kitchen, and find someplace to hide. With Brigit's Pass without a Trace! Fen goes to tackle the crane, succeeds! And they tumble in the room. The crane makes eye contact with the puffin, and they come into the room. TEAM BIRD Sprocket wants to see the sights because it was super hard to get here and we're probably not coming back. They head to Nimbus where the hanging gardens - the two pyramids on top of each other. There are some parts of the pyramid missing and only air genasi are going in and out with bountiful food, and crystals. There's also a shop - Erlenmeyer's Flask. Tezka heads to the alchemist's There's also a storm giant blacksmith Thestrus. Sprocket is now taking the role of servant, and Stalwart is the lord. These two. Thestrus is not having fun making baby weapons. He wants to make giant weapons. Anything magical? His magnum opus - a massive battle axe! It could fall Jack. THUS THE NAMESAKE OF THE TOWN JACKFALL If Stalwart can lift the sword with one hand, he can have it. He tries! It barely moves, it's really too long for the other side to pick up. But the blacksmith is impressed. Sprocket heads to the opposite side to help! And can't budge it. At freaking all. (NAT 1) The blacksmith loses it and Sprocket challenges the blacksmith to pull it. The blacksmith trips and falls on the anvil and chaos ensues. (PORTENT ROLL 1) The blacksmith never thought that Stalwart would be able to lift the shaft, and he pulls out a secret sword - a broadsword or longsword. Splinter - a small chink out of the side of the axe. He never thought anyone would be worthy, but Stalwart has deemed himself worthy! It has a faint green edge on the side, and down the center of the blade. Sprocket identifies the sword. It's a longsword of plant slaying. It really shines against plants. It's 1000 electrum. And he passes. Stalwart and Sprocket head to the alchemist and meet Tezka again with tons of potions of healing. Into Erlenmeyer's Flask - there's some weird ass potions A quite old genasi by how hard the wind is blowing on her. There's tons of potions in vials and glass behind her - and they're all on tap. The two S's make Tezka be their servant, to bring them to the proprietor and skip the line. They want to see the private collection - the reserve. They walk down a spiral staircase - where the smoke is coming from. And they take some poison damage. There's a large glass case pillar with a bubbling green potion. There's one thing that matters most - her hand appears on the inside and pulls it out. On her pinky is a diamond. It's a marvelous pigment. One can create anything if anyone is so inclined. 20,000 electrum. FUCK. You have wasted our time, and they leave. She threatens them not to waste her time again. Stalwart notices a small parcel on the ground - to Lorelai, but they don't touch it. TEAM DIPLOMAT Hiding in the kitchen! Fenn is dealing with an angry crane ROLL FOR INITIATIVE The crane slams a glass on Fenn's head. Fenn steps back, casts Rope Trick and books it into Extradimensional Space, gets hit, but makes it and he disappears. The puffin comes in - confused as to why his boss is in the kitchen. The puffin grabs Fen's blood, and says to go find Lorelei  - they're here. The crane calls for Minnie - Minnie opens up her cloak and a batlike wing falls out and takes the blood. Varys is itching to get at this fiend, but Randall and Brigit convince him silently to hold off and stay hidden for now. Minnie disappears and says she's on it. The crane has left the kitchen. Brigit, Varis and Randall head out through the side door. Fen releases after some time, takes his blood and leaves. Out the side door - trash cans full of food. Which is strange because we've seen a famine basically everywhere. We circle back around to the front of the casino/hotel. Minnie appears in front of Fen and attempts to charm him. Fen is filled with energy and emotional rage. Minnie says "it's time to go for a swim" Back to the entrance - lots of people are leaving because of the wild hogs. Brigit gets the key from Mr. Tux, leaves word for Fen and heads to our rooms. The common room is barren. (Rooms 8,9 and 10) Randall - what happened? There's another bard in the hotel. A prick from his high school - Lorelei. Don't let Nyx or Tezka see her as their true form. She went down guest services and did something to his pan flute - partial silence. But he fixes it. And she's got this pen - a giant red fish and uses it to hide. We catch up. When is everyone supposed to get back? Randall sends a message to Tezka. Randall practices and immediately sound flawless. TEAM BIRD Tezka is frantic - we've got to get back to the Roost. But do they want the sword? LONG ASS DISCUSSION They'll come back later. Performance flying checks: Sprocket - 2. Stalwart - 17. Heads through Laputa Town to note the shops - Earlyworm café, Booksworth academy, the rookery (single solitary tower, open at the top), the library of paradise. They get to The Roost - notice that the owl guards have been through the ringer, shirts are ripped and there's a bit of chaos about. They head to guest services - they're looking for some guests that are friends of ours. And also they get a free room for having Randall never play again (5 and 6) And Randall has 8. The common room is deserted - only employees at this point. On top of a fiend infestation, there's also a boar infestation. Varis is paying attention, hears the birds and notices that they're outside, but they're birds, so Randall jumps up and grabs the rest of the party. BACK TOGETHER AND IT FEELS SO GOOD SHORT REST Brigit locate's person on Fen, he's straight downstairs… Godie comes by and tells us there is a special on the casino - half-ante tonight only, but we have to go rescue Fen, so we shrug him off. Should have jumped him. Shit. Oh well, heading downstairs. Sprocket can see invisible people with his third eye, but Godie is hanging back, waiting for us to come down. Stalwart looks around - every single owl guard and servant is desecrated - under the influence of an evil being. Sprocket clicks his mage hand into existence, flies it over by Godie and has it point down and then winks at Varis. Stalwart charges. ROLL FOR INITIATIVE Stalwart nails it (NAT 20), slamming him on the ground. Hits and hits and hits and hits, causing a shit-ton of radiant damage. There's a sense of the invisibility being disturbed, but it holds firm. Varis shoots his bow at the fiend, hitting again and again, he's up, but that's dumb. Brigit turns into a wolf, bites his neck, but then holds it to make him lose consciousness instead of ripping it to shreds. Stalwart ties him up with his sailor-ness. Brigit noses him across her back and carries him with us. Sprocket pours ink over the incubus. To the guest services Princess' pen is currently empty for some reason Down and down as we get down to the pool …. Meanwhile… TEAM Fuck yo shit up Poor poor Fen has been charmed before and if he were able, would not like this right now. The bond is somewhat comforting - Minnie is relaxing as they traverse into the pool area. No light sources down here - Fen has excellent darkvision, and fades to invisible in the darkness. A creature with too long hands and teeth and pure evil looks up from the pool, happy that Minnie has brought another sacrifice(?) Minnie kisses Fen Max health is reduced. The long tendril arm of the creature touches his forehead, intelligence saving throw. Every muscle seizes and is stunned for an entire minute. It offers a deal - (1) go upstairs, (2) you spoke with Euclid, and Fen feels a false memory entering his mind. He's going to go upstairs, march to the bellhop station, go to the palace and bring someone here - the water genasi woman. He has a false memory of a great owl telling him the end of the world Gar'daemon is occurring and the only way to save the world is to bring the water genasi princess downstairs. Your trigger - when the jack falls, you charge. UNTIL NEXT TIME

Fen looks for a book on brewing - a human book with some gnomish writing on it that discussing brewing with Roots - A ROOT BEER Hidleson Green of Shroomtop Sprocket and Fen head to the market to buy ingredients for sample alcohol to sell Windhaven has changed a bit. There's so many wares now to be sold at the harvest festival. But there's still drab all around, the city seems to have acclimated to their presence though Brigit pushes a drab into an alley semi-steakily to search the drab's body for a spell written on his skin. Last name - Hilbrant. Part of a family. There is a tattoo underneath his beard of a sun. And a sun pendant, which no one has taken. Brigit takes said pendant. Fen, Sprocket and Brigit go shopping for brewery ingredients and Brigit buys some diamonds and Sprockets sells a giant opal And if we get a chance to swing by the Magister's Circle, Eon, the alabaster seat isn't doing well There's going to be an election process to pick the next one. The Emerald Veil Randall casts Legend Lore on Summer and is creating a narrative performance. Followed by marigolds wherever she walked. She still exists to this day. Just avoid the tale of the demon prince of pleasure. So Randall is going to make a song of hope - Marigold Wine. Varis is chilling in the bar Varis meets a beige ninja. He wants to trade a tale for a bottle of wine. Matthew Windsmith He double checks to see if this is Reynaldine, but it's not. But he's definitely not human either. It's an air genasi. An elven woman, drunken, heads up to Varis and includes him in the conversation. Asks to tell him a tale. She's from Tower Bridge! And he suggests that she could maybe go to the Druid's Grove in Var'Taos. OOOH she knows Yvonne Finally, he talks to the bouncer. Itori, from Monethern. She didn't want to live in her mother's shadow, in her mother's expectations. She's from the Veritasia Palace. Randall lands Heads to the inn REAL QUICK - Brigit flies up and tries to locate the seal 3 times every 2000 ft, doesn't find it anywhere REAL QUICK - Fen gets a ride to Cloud City early tomorrow - Sun's first light, but he'll get back by sundown. The Storm Peaks. To the tavern Randall is greeted by some the Umbertown Kaval of Horns in the tavern - they wish to hear another tale from him, just not about a pegasus Randall also lets all of us know about the marigold wine. MARKETING Randall performs the 2 doctors. And ensures that Sprocket is aware that this song is about him and Stalwart. Sprocket has no idea. But Randall can't tell if it's because he's distracted or not. Brigit recognizes and starts crying. Whichever doctor the crowd responds to best - the tall doctor - drinks a glass of marigold wine. NAT 20 TIPS He discusses the performance with a fellow bard - who says that dancing is all the rage nowadays. Upstairs We discuss the alcohol We're going to make a marigold wine and a orange, apricot beer Brigit takes cuttings from the ingredients to make labels tomorrow. SKILL CHALLENGE Sprocket runs the shots and lets everyone know what to do at the exact times Fen is smashing up the ingredients and chopping Varis taste tests along the way and ensures we don't poison ourselves or burn the inn down BUT HE FAILS. He's been drinking too much today. We have one less bottle of wine. Brigit and Randall and some eagles and the Pegasus take the beer up to the stratosphere to cool the alcohol Overall success, we have 5 bottles of wine, 6 bottles of beer Flying down, we see a griffin land. He wants to meet Randall for a joust tomorrow at sundown. Varis, Brigit and Randall go to bed Brigit makes vines and flowers on the bottles, but she's a little drunk, so tomorrow. Harvest Day IPA The Return of Summer Marigold Wine BUT WAIT we need to age the wine Fen and Sprocket head out Fen and Sprocket's Adventure The town has changed in preparation - a paper lantern display The librarian is of course inside the library But be careful to not mention her to Leonidas as it is almost the anniversary of her death. Sprocket is able to turn his third eye to Sylvan, he sees flowerbuds in the crack of the hinge. The writing says "So long as last summer's light exists, I shall stay for you my queen." Sprocket and Fen see it's cracking and he's able to walk through the door The sun has set in his sanctum Sprocket pushes Fen forward Leonidas is not okay. He's sad. What's wrong - It's over, he's won. And now assassins have killed Spring. The Feywilde is lost, there will be no festival tomorrow. There's tea party tomorrow - we shouldn't miss it maybe? Leonidas shames Fen for not visiting his brother in Monethern after saving him. HE'S GONNA DO IT The abomination will be undone and the evil within will run free. Fuck He's in a jail. Tomorrow's events will free him though Two days from now will be his victory day, as it has happened before. Windhaven will not last - it was founded on a promise. So then.  What's next? Leonidas only knows through tomorrow, because tomorrow he dies He saw a timeline change once - in the trial of Fen. His sister lies dormant in the sunless citadel He's never seen past tomorrow. It is destiny. It's too late for anything now, but he'll see us again sometime. He owes some friends of theirs a favor. Fen stays for a bit and tries to cheer him up with tales of Evelon. They take the Return of Summer and drink 3 glasses - to the inevitable They head down Stalwart lets it slip that the Festival might not be happening. And someone is going to escape from jail. The guard is having a rough time TO SLEEP TO DAY Randall tries to get some information from his followers on the Queen of Thorns He gets back 3 rumors - 2 false, one true. (1) The queen of thorns has a band of emerald cloak bards who have taken up residence and will be at the tea party (2) She has a pet white rabbit who is always late (3) She loves the color red Brigit heads to the glass etchers Commune with Nature - there's a ton of caves and natural stuff underneath. Powerful beings - there's been quite movement here. Pretty powerful fey here. And the nature felt Leonidas for once - he's leaving. The fiends are that bad. But there's a shitton of celestials down below windmill willow. 200+ Influence from other planes of existence - someone else opened up the windmill willow and walk through it. They were kind of moldy. And it felt like death. Brigit meets two teenagers- Kima and Francois. Greater restores the drab dad, the brewer of the beer books. He's back for 10 seconds. They understandably freak and are so happy. Brigit can only do this once a day, but she's going to try to come back tomorrow. She wishes she could do more, but in the meantime assures them they're doing all they can taking care of their dad. They give her root to immediately age alcohol. Bottles etched for free for good deeds. Hoorah. Varis and Randall head to Westrun fields and there are murders and murders of crows. They look like scarecrows from the sky, but something is weird. Varis is basically invisible. He sees a little girl carrying a pail of flowers. And yes, these are zombies. Her dog sniffs Varis out though - a giant husky, Balto. Aeris Westrun. She picks flowers for the market. Alright yeah, so this is actually her family. The flowers stopped growing after her great grandpa tried to grow flowers with the same bouquet of flowers grown from the same soil, so now they grow flowers in people/zombies whatever. Randall heads down to help talk to the kid. These flowers are unnaturally grown; from a magical sense She is alone except for Balto. THERE'S A GUY THAT GUYS FLOWERS, ONE FOR EACH FINGER AND HE BUYS 6 EVERY TIME MOTHERFUCKER He'll be back in 2 weeks. He comes by every 6 weeks. Varis hands her 50 gold and has her put the 6-fingered man on a blacklist. And weirdly, Lorelei owns this estate, Randall's employee Varis takes a look at one of the zombies - there's a gravestone. Barnibus Westrun and his dog spot. Most have their dogs buried beneath them. Varis and Randall head to the masoleum No undead, but it still includes a man and his dog. Jesepth Westrun and Winklebottom. With a Jester's hat underneath the name LEGEND LORE He serves the clery estate and their field is sterile, but a bloom of flowers grows in anyone that is planted in their field. They won the first harvest festival and he used the winnings for the next year, but he cheated. So he was cursed. Winklebottom now serves as the footstool of the Queen and will forever. Randall turns a sword into a circlet with a blacksmith, but she keeps the handle Fen's adventure at Storm Peaks The farmer's daughters are super hungover Back to the library at Storm's Peaks There's a ton of information here about Gardammon and the Zodiacs There's no information about the location of the zenith's, except for the one above Macchu, the Forest Zenith. But there was one that fell and became Tygras Logath. And notice, there's a HUGE crater where the last zenith fell at Tygras Logath Weirdly, there is no origin of the Zeniths And Xyn came by later to check out the book Fen did, but obviously, Fen already had it. And just as of note, there were a lot of fires down below in the last fire. And she's shut it down since the fire, even though a lot of students weren't visiting There's a god of destruction from the petal that has already been destroyed - the initiator of aboleth's and beholder's and mind flayer's. Therezadoon. The chained god of madness, he who walks below And there's a corpse of an earth elemental that makes up Bael'vun The harvest zenith was closed at the destruction of Uma, the matrim pact was made so that it would never been opened again. And the pact was made in the shadow of the zenith itself. Fen gets a 10% return on his investment at the Storm's Peaks bank. ## Chapter 69T -- The 6 Finger of Life
**Author**: Brigit
**Party Track**: Tieflings

### What Happened

TLDR We left the Windmill Willow, had a harrowing experience with Tygras Firebrand, one of the progenitors. We killed him last time, but he has somehow been reborn (due to his fiendness). We had some issues getting familiars back with the party due to the storm of Tempest being released. Outside the Traveler's Pony (Going back to the height of the festival) We enter on Knob - a gnomish artificer with a racoon mecha-companion, Scamp. He's come to the festival to scour through garbage to further his inventions. He's heard rumors that The Pegasus' group stayed in the third floor and they were tinkering all night long. Knob throws up his hood of elven kind and tries to stay undetected - successfully. And they head to the eastern side of the building, to where they can get in undetected. He peeks into the second floor - mostly abandoned due to people leaving the city. And downstairs there's the sound of something breaking. The third floor - Knob uses his thieves' tools to open up the door. There should have been a fireworks show by now, but a huge gust of wind from an explosion, it seems, rocks the inn instead. He's in a very fine room - a room made up for a dignitary - very nice linens, and flowers with a card (apologizing for how check-in was, and offering a free night) The walls begin to shake violently - like in a hurricane Scamp went a little lockjaw, but he seems okay. Except that it seems like his memory has been erased, he doesn’t recognize knob and is speaking very differently than before. He is supposed to be a human. His name is Rya. Scamp is fine, but Rya is taking refuge in this vessel for now. Rya believes they're in grave danger, and they need to find somewhere safe from the wind. Aka not here. Knob unlocks a briefcase and takes a look at photos and pink crystal; it's some kind of device but nothing like Knob has seen before. Knob takes everything and puts it in not-Scamps back, who is now walking on two legs instead of the usual 4 Knob jumps on his mecha and rides out the way they came. Back outside - the bottom stories are now burning. But he does see a strange 8ft blue dragonborn creature A sunny glade We march forward led by a white spirit where Irethia used to be The rangers note that the spirits around them look like Mahen And we head upwards through some pouring rain A strange land A young woman steps forward in a heavenly white light - asking for the knight of the dusken wood. ### Key Plot Points
- We left the Windmill Willow, had a harrowing experience with Tygras Firebrand, one of the progenitors. We killed him last time, but he has somehow been reborn (due to his fiendness).
- We march forward led by a white spirit where Irethia used to be
- And we head upwards through some pouring rain
- A young woman steps forward in a heavenly white light - asking for the knight of the dusken wood.
- Varis steps forward and she bows to him, trying to determine his intentions
- Varis is wary from the fight, but not unwelcoming entirely
- Irethia was the first of their kind to escape, but that was a big exception
- Fen and Sprocket munch down spell slot candies
- The wooden carving has maybe something to do with a ritual, but Knob doesn't know for sure
- The two will be rewarded handsomely for their help and their dark signs will be removed.

### Characters Involved
Brigit, Randall, Sprocket, Fen, Varis, Knob, Scamp, Volstead, Mahen, Bigsby, Finger, Rya, Haste, Arcane, Jolt

- "I found another"

A"

- "BURNS IT TO THE GROUND"

### Intrigue/Foreshadowing
- We left the Windmill Willow, had a harrowing experience with Tygras Firebrand, one of the progenitors. We killed him last time, but he has somehow been reborn (due to his fiendness).
- Knob takes everything and puts it in not-Scamps back, who is now walking on two legs instead of the usual 4
- But he does see a strange 8ft blue dragonborn creature
- Her kind were transported to the fey wilde and now there's a momentary gap due to the harvest zenith opening.

Heading downward to a modern looking courtroom Many people are giving Sprocket a strange look - scorn and curiosity, because he eliminated Umbara Populi. Structure: Defendant and prosecutor get to summon 3 character witnesses who must produce some evidence. And they also must also produce a representative (aka lawyer) to give evidence to court. A beholder, Erlex, is produced as the Leon's representative. Lorelei appears, ready to help. Erlex is going to represent Leon. And Randall is going to represent Sprocket. Of note: Randall is in the competition and by representing Sprocket, he's showing comradery and that this trial is above the competition. In the back, a tiefling is brought forward in chains, and it looks to be Anemone's younger sister. Ruth announces the beginning of the trial. The trial begins And the judge drawls, he believes in a world where all wizards serve the rightful ruler of the magister's circle. My Name Is Sprocket Overspark is accused of the crime of necromancy by Leon Grimwald. First witness - Olivia Nominae. The beholder's eye opens and a light shines upon her, blemishes form on her skin and her inherent glamours fade away with his anti-magic field Of note: Erlax is who we're battling next trial. First witness - Olivia She's Anemone's sister Please provide her evidence. She is a necromancer. And many people in the crowd shout out, a necromancer and a tiefling, that's why they can't be trusted. Let's put her on trial, the scum! But Anemone didn't know she was a necromancer. How did she manage to hide and how was she found out? Every necromancer has some cover up for what their normal spells are. Sometimes we have entirely new spellbooks. The spellbook is encoded to look like it's filled with normal spells. In reality, the real spellbook is used every day instead of the fake one. The necromancer spellbook needs to be maintained, but she failed to cast gentle repose and she was found out when she was being questioned by the magister's circle and her spellbook fell apart. Sprocket, show your spellbook. A hush falls over the crowd. Does it look familiar? No, and no, it's not her spellbook If she could inspect it, could she tell it's necromancy? But Sprocket stops her, and says it was cursed by someone else. Sprocket admits that it is covered in necromancer energy now, but it was not yesterday. Anemone is freaking out. This doesn't sound right, and Olivia is going to die. She turns to Brigit to try to help, and they go to the council. Maybe, we at least give Anemone and her sister a time to talk. But, by approaching, they're accepting that one of us is the next witness. Since, Anemone doesn't believe that her sister is a necromancer at all, we should call her to the stand. Defense first witness - Anemone How does she respond to these baseless allegations We're very close. We were together at the magister's circle. They murdered the college of glamour and now they're going against my sister. Why did you even detain her. Anemone never knew her sister as a necromancer. Sprocket can't be a necromancer, he's a good guy. For the third trial, she had to rely on a stranger because her sister was nowhere to be found, already baselessly taken by you lot. What about Leon? He went through first in the portal. And it's really weird Sil was a prisoner. While Anemone just strode on by and these aberration-esque creatures didn’t' even touch him. … Did you ever see your sister perform necromancy? No But if anyone had, it would be you? Yes. What about discrimination from the magister's circle? Tieflings are hunted and the leader of the bardic college of glamour were murdered. Anemone taught her sister magic and the only person that could have taught her magic was her. Wait. Anemone taught her sister all she knows, and she's not a necromancer. Anemone should produce her spellbook the council She doesn't have a spellbook, just a set list. She lists out all the spells she has. Leon smiles - points at a specific spell, modify memory This seems to be her full set list right? And Andrea agrees. But if she wanted to remove a spell from memory, modify memory would do it. Anemone's never casted modify memory on her. But she did show it to her sister. Like three years ago. All that it would take is a remove curse or greater restoration. Ursala pipes up for the first time, she can cast the greater restoration to see if modify memory was ever cast upon Anemone. And we agree. Insight, Randall (but not many other people), Leon looks ecstatic. Ursala casts the spell And Anemone begins crying. Olivia is a necromancer and I have nothing else to say. She's done. Anemone and Olivia do their secret handshake. Anemone comes back, leans over, talks to Brigit. Half the council is corrrupted. They were going to use her as a patsy and kill her, and now she walks with a limp. Randall notices that Ursala casted Revivify, plus there is a dreamcatcher at her waist, and some form of spirit was revived. Wait - what about that limp. It's a battle scar from her old fight with her sister. Uber lie. But we believe her Prosecution second witness - Regalia takes over the body of Ursala Tell your story She's the radiant spirit of the rabbit. She's been murdered in the demi plane Of note - Sprocket feels a sense of kinship towards Regalia. Sil may be alive, but Regalia was captured and came into contact with another who had another radiant spirit - Fen Darkeyes. He was there while she was being tortured. What held you there? A soul jar spell. It's used by necromancer's to capture and enslave spirits. Could you identify someone who has casted that spell? She looks at Sprocket and sees that yes. He has been a vessel of a soul jar spell in the last 24 hours. (The raven queen, thank you varis) Sprocket doesn’t make sense of this, this is the first he's hearing of this. And it would mean soul jar is in Sprocket's spellbook. But Sprocket currently can't read his spellbook, so maybe Leon can. Leon takes the book with him to the council. We object and he gives the book to Lilith, that she should read page 3. Sprocket calls out that this dude read a necromancy spell book in front of the entire court. Anything else? She wants to talk to Argent and demands his immediate release. Compromise! He's going to take control of Fen's body. Argent Ha! You call me?!? I've never been called as a witness. Are you a prisoner. Yes, but no? He's a business partner, but they were fighting against a necromancer. I was summoned like you and then I was captured. And end Regalia. Defense second witness - Leon. Calling Leon to enter the zone of truth Lorelei "got em!" Do you believe Sprocket is a necromancer? He does believe that he was. Has his spellbook been tampered without his knowledge It was tampered with his knowledge Are you framing Sprocket? Yes. But not without good reason? I believe he's a necromancer What's the reason? He has an informant who mentioned that Sprocket purchased a spell from a library in the city formerly known as Cloud City If anyone in the council had necromancy spells, would that make them a necromancer Sprocket - no. Material does not a necromancer make. Is that the only evidence? Also his spellbook is filled with necromancy spells. But Leon did not have influence, it's all Sprocket's all undoing. He might have failed a deal of some kind. To be a necromancer you have to cast necromancy spells. Has Leon ever cast a necromancy spell? <silence> Randall <to the council>: Must be a yes. What about the council, do they believe this? Thesolonias - there is a singular necromancer, but he wants to know more. Does he have more than one spellbook? No Would he submit it for examination? Yes Any more doubt? Elmer - not one, but two necromancers, and Leon, the one who is on the council,  is the main one, and both on trial? (Elmer seems to be highly suspicious that we have 2/4 people left in the trial under suspicious (Sprocket/Leon) and "we're"(Randall) trying to kick the "outsiders" out - aka Leon/Sprocket Elmer believes that Randall's trying to knock people out of the trial, so it's just him and Anemone. Lilith is glued to the spell book. Her lips have decayed to a pale purple. And she didn't turn that last page. It's a curse of some kind. Randall calls attention to Lilith's curse. Andrea was going to say something, that she believed them, but Lilith is fucked right now. The Beholder points at Lilith. Sprocket's spellbook is attuned to someone else. And he takes a big hit in HP. Lilith snaps out of it through the beholder's light. This spellbook is cursed. I believe Sprocket. Where did you get this and why do you have it? Sprocket - the original was my spellbook, but yesterday it turned into this. I bought a spell at a library and it must have started then, but I don't know what happened yesterday. Did Sprocket cast and bought a clone spell? He's just learning that clone can do that to his spellbook. And they're going to have to talk about his membership. Lilith is exhausted and she could have died. And Sprocket's going to help bring that attempter to justice. Lilith feels betrayal at Leon, and Sprocket. This trial was brought about by the feylord. What about the demiplane? They're useful pocket dimensions. <enter long winded nonsense here> He hosted the last trial? Yes. Who was there, was there a necromancer? No necromancer, other than Sprocket. Who's Edward Zail? Black market supplier of stolen goods He's never met him. Except he kind of has. Could you take a look, and tell us what you understand of its origins? He checks with Sprocket. S - It's not even really his, be my guest. R casts something akin to Legend Lore This is a spellbook of sorcerous necromancy. A very powerful magical artifact. It did belong to an old necromancer - Mordred. FUCK Sprocket is bound to this book. It's almost a phylactery, but not as potent. It would turn Sprocket into a lich. R asks about the 6 finger. Sprocket doesn't have one, because he cut it off. He's going to have to hold onto this spellbook if he doesn't want to be possessed by it. Now, Lilith has also attuned to the book. So, one of them must die, or we find a loophole, or Mordred is coming back. <pow wow> Talking to Reyardine Xyn has taken over for Gabriel And he's looking for Nyx. Xyn's also looking for her sister. Could Sprocket be the necromancer in the future? Yes, if nothing else is done. Varis takes a look at the council members - Leon, Ursala, hayden, lilith and anemone are all undead to him (and Sil but she's undead, and not here) Defense third witness - Ruth Do you know Sprocket Of course, we go way back. I was saved from him from a terrible fate. I was held captive by a necromantic hat. I've been one of the many witches and wizards saved by him. We provided prophesies to them about Gard'daemon. Randall notices - Lilith cares but is concerned about herself, still needs more for everyone. What about Sil? Her and Sheila found kinship with Sil, and kind of Ursala. Sil is a friend and confidant and we went with her to the third trial. Hayden agreed to go through the teleportation scroll with us. The radiant spirit took over Sil. Regalia was different. It might have affected Ursala. Sil was self-sacrificing and charming But All hell broke loose once we went through the teleportation. Something happened and she was in that cell for a day She talked to Varis and agreed to help her. Ruth heard she'd be saved by another. Was Sil in the cell? No. I was alone Did you see her at all? I might have, but. I saw her in a vision. Alone in a dark place, and she'd never felt more alone. Everyone looks at Ursala If she was a hostage, of course she felt that way. She's resting. And slow at a morkoth, but it passes Fen passes the dex save and casts Lightning arrow at Leon. And something falls to the floor. On his face is an ancient arcane symbol emblazed on his face. Varis looks closely and gets a good read on it. There's a lack of malicious intent on it, but it's close to a death ward. And Leon seems happy to have died. It seems like Leon might have someone disappeared out of his body right before he died. Then Brigit sets up some eagles as a rearguard, (who is having an interesting interactions with the peryton that do not get along) Then uses Lorelei to get to the front of the crowd Goes into a rage and begins getting people moving out the door Varis throws out some arrows in celebration and truly emphasizes that the necromancer is dead i.e. chunks of the body are left with a big red stain And additionally, steps into the ethereal plane to take a look around. And sees a secret chest! Behind Leon. Loot!!! And also sees the same rune. Lorelei's going to inspire people and countercharm as necessary. We made it! Valley of Anchors Ruth thanks Sprocket saves her for twice in one day. Sprocket needs to find the party and find Lilith to deal with this curse situation. Ruth starts to contact a paladin friend at Conquis Abbey to take care of this Randall books it out the door HEYO A little guilt-ridden, but takes a hard look at the door More and more people start crowding out. And everyone gets out and Varis is last. Fen and Randall can hear from the box - there's something heavy on the inside. A body perhaps Varis - necromancer's don't die well and they keep coming back. So we should probably kill whatever's inside this as well. Or throw it into the bottom of the ocean. Or down a volcano. A few minutes go by and the door dissipates and Lilith and Urlex walk out. Lilith has the book and a ring on her finger. Urlax leaves to hang out at the top of the tree and we'll see him tomorrow for the 5th trial To set the stage It's been a long fucking day Twilight is setting and everyone is rounded up in groups licking their wounds. The masses have surrounded Lorelei as she plays music Talking to Lilith Sprocket realizes that there are ramifications when you use a necromantic spellbook and apologizes to Lilith for getting her wrapped up in this mess. Sprocket explains our theory - we think that since Sprocket spent more time with the book, cleansing him is going to be a much harder task than cleansing Lilith. Lilith focuses with Augury and tries to figure out what's going to happen when the curse is broken. She grabs three post it notes and they begin to burn in her hand as her third eye opens. Weal! She believes this plan will work. Sprocket considers this curse some form of cosmic punishment for how he's been acting these past 6 months. So. Let's do this. Also, of note, Lilith is proud of him and decides to not remove his silver seat status. Sprocket receives back the necromancer spellbook and an Atlas of Endless Horizons. Fen recognizes that Lilith is wearing a ring of mind shielding And asks her to hand over the ring He doesn't trust that ring because it's Leon's She hands it over and Varis smacks Fen with Protection from Evil and then puts on the ring Brigit casts Greater Restoration on Lilith and it works! But she feels awful. In much need of some soup and a long rest. She goes to check in with the people to see who they want their champion to be. Randall greater restores Sprocket to give him back HPs Fen puts on the ring And talks to Regalia She gives him some insight into Argent - he's the bloodthirstyiest one of them all She is the zodiac of the rabbit She was going to win and get the ability to guide magic on this plane. Poor knob. Good thing he can be rebuilt, and we'll pay for it. Inside, undisturbed are numerous jars with red liquid and a body. It's Umbara! And a hefty amount of gold. Now what? Drop the force cage Brigit's going to go run to get Thesolonias, Ruth, Lilith and Elmer Varis goes to dig around the cage Knob gathers up scamp. Varis notices these jars are pieces of somebody but not Umbara. The sigil is shined on everything And it's been teleported everywhere without a lot of protection AND 800 platinum The bottom of the chest is also soaked with blood of Umbara Randall/Sprocket see there's a magic circle underneath to hold Umbara in a permanent form of stasis that he would have had to agreed to It's lined everything with glyphs of warding to hold Umbara and these jars within this plane Umbara has wounds, but he looks okay Brigit gets back with the platinum seats we trust Elmer takes a solid look at the runes and starts deciphering them But hasn't seen anything like this in 600 years - no one's seen that symbol since the trials started. Very faintly, her heart is still beating. It seems like the diadem has been found and is being used. Stopping Gard'daemon is your destiny. Nyx holds the diadem and she must make the wish before the 6th trial. The raven queen said he was a messenger for her. Sprocket looks around Elmer is looking at Thesolonias Thesolonias is confused as fuck Lilith is stone cold and slack jawed at Sprocket You are wrapped up in all sort of things The covenant was formed to give order and civility to arcane magic And she might understand who these jars belong to They belong to Sil They can't die before the sixth trial Leon's soul might be at the sixth trial. Nyx pulls on her earring and throws down a smoke bomb around herself. A worried looking Tezka comes running out of the monastery and has turned from scarlet to a pale purple. Pieces Diadem of Dawn Nyx's patron was Summer, Julianna So she's probably gone to the Feywilde To Tezka Tell us what you know about diadem nyx just took. At rager at valley of anchors. Miss you Diadem can change course of fate, will burn out user, nyx betrayed my trust. On my way. So how can Tezka get here? Teleportation circles with the qualashahar's and magister's circle. RAGER With Lorelei Fen also takes some time to chat. She feels both in and out of her element. Be careful what you wish for She still can't figure out who set her up. This card has brought her so much bad, it got her arrested, she got put on trial, but the connection got her thrown into the biggest tournament. She didn't even have to participate to ultimately win this thing She wants to rule, but she doesn't want to waste away and die. She wants to be able to do something worthwhile. Going up against Randall and Sprocket isn't going to be good, because they're more powerful (secret) She loves having people love her, but she's scared of the magical overload involved in the position. Ultimately she thinks it would be cool to combine the two teleportation network … She has a bit of resentment in her eyes from the feylords, she's not incompetent, but she's just trying to figure out what she wants to do. She's excited and scared. They just hope that someone makes it onto the seat that they can trust. Oh and if she makes it through, she's not going to follow through the persecution of Olivia. Yay! She's learned a lot through traveling with Randall (also secret) Get some sleep, I've got to join my own party THE RAGER Randall corners the platinum seats and wants to learn more about the Alabaster Seat. Btw, Elmer loves the tower of wine glasses The alabaster seat is 'our' boss, leading the platinum seats with autonomy in your decisions (although appeasing people as necessary) (Thesolonias) Also, you have the burden of carrying and being a conduit of magical energy. It's how arcane power is dolled out. It is the final act of the magister's tournament, and that all began with the Last Curator himself. We believe the origins are the progenitors, but it's uncertain where they got it from. If it wasn't passed on… many have tried, but it always returns to the resting place upon their death, back into the valley until a new champion is chosen Magic comes from the rose, the barrier that surrounds this realm. Without an alabaster seat, anything surrounding the barriers would be able to come in. And also the emerald vale also takes care of those beings that want to enter this realm. A magical firewall Would it be possible to open up the floodgates? He didn't used to think so, but those Morkoth's keep slipping through. Probably due to the lack of an Alabaster seat right now. He's not sure how Leon handled that, but they should not have been here. They haven't been on this plane for more than a century. Could a construct take it on? Maybe? We haven't tried. Historically, it's fallen upon those with living, beating hearts. Enjoy your tower, Wizards love towers. Big mystery If we don't have hearts, do we bleed? Do we need to breathe? Where are our special powers Before we go to bed Brigit sets up an alarm while letting the beer brew Randall requests a servant to bring him a pillow and blanket Fen lets us know that Lorelei is a little power hungry Let's get drunk AF. We all find a brief moment of peace and revelry after a seriously hellish day. The next morning. For flavor - Randall went to bed in the middle of a pile of falling glasses. Waking up to the crunch of broken glass, he wakes up in what used to be the wine tower before Elmer broke it. And calls for a hangover robe. Additionally a pile of clothing is waiting by the doors for Randall Brigit tries to go outside, but the doors are locked Heads over to Giddeon, doesn't want to touch him, so pokes him with a fork. He wakes up yelling, waking up everyone in the ballroom. Brigit tells her companions to go outside and begins setting up the Hero's feast in the morning light. Hero’s feast: It's going to be a somewhat primitive feast, think very natural with a lot of barbarian flair. I want it to be on the ground by the tree, in the morning dawn light. There's a small campfire in the middle with some smouldering herbs in it to produce an earthy, spiced smoke. Not spicy, but just to make it a little more festive, like rosemary, sage. There's food strewn about in a rough circle, with really basic, mostly non-processed food of the forest - fruits, mushrooms, nuts, maybe some cheeses and tons of bread and a big wild boar's leg. And some ale and wine. Basically a barbarian's charcuterie board. Brigit creates a poultice of mud and berries for war paint on her face and hands and arms (like henna) in addition to the meal if anyone else wants some war paint. And I also want some special ceremonial bracelets and anklets that have little bells and bones and dried herbs on them. Erlax comes up and is interested in the hero's feast, Brigit offers some war paint if he wants - draws shit tons of beholder eyes on him For that, he has a secret for her. Sprocket tells Giddeon that 30 years has past, he knows this is very sudden, but Sprocket had to tell him. And as they walk outside, the magnificent mansion diminishes. Sprocket has good news though, and they step outside to look around for Lorelei So of note, Lorelei is a half elf and Giddeon is a human And Sprocket can't quite remember his wife's name. But knows that it's an elven woman who he married much to the chagrin of his parents. So instead, Sprocket asks Lorelei what her mother's name is - Elevestra. And then introduces the daughter/father He was gone for awhile, but he's back now and I needed to make sure you knew each other. Lorelei is stoked, but Giddeon didn't know his wife was pregnant. Lorelei, what a lovely name. Lorelei thought for the longest time she was the last Clary. And also asks if it was a sabotage, but no, this is not a classic Sprocket practical joke, and then explains how we found Giddeon. No matter what, Lorelei will make whoever took away her father pay. And Giddeon's going to join Lorelei's team as he begins to tell her the tale of how he met her mother. Brigit takes the last one to study and grow in the grove Revisited squads for the trial - Anemone, Olivia and Brigit TEAM FLY - Randall, Knob and Varis Sprocket, Fen and Oswald Oswald is weirded out by the platinum seats not all sitting together, Brigit explains the rift created by Leon, so Oswald heads to talk to all of them, he calls out to the ship for a barrel of ale A turban headed person hands down a barrel of ale Brigit believes it to be Dune, but Randall looks up to see that it's not a Genasi, but is instead a Vendelkan, Goodman. (Varis - I think it is actually a Tuesday) All four anchor stones of light up on each side But before, we give some part of ourselves to Randall for safe keeping. PREP TIME Randall appears and has some time to set up before the Beholder is there - he's in a sand pit with some statues around, which weirdly seem to depict other people. ## Chapter 79T -- The chamber of blood and voting' Colts, contributions, and coagulation in the colosseum caldera
**Author**: Brigit
**Party Track**: Tieflings

### What Happened

TLDR We had a small moment of fresh air and shenanigans ensued in a wonderful party full of too much alcohol and too many drunk magic users. We had a heroes' feast. Reunited Gideon and Lorelei We checked in with Nyx and Tezka went off to go to the feywilde to try and stop her after she stole the diadem of the dawn We split up into 3 (technically 4 groups). Sprocket, Fen, Oswald Varis, Randall, Knob Lorelei, Gideon and an unknown third. Anemone, Brigit and Olivia COMBAT, Top of Round 3 with Randall Randall blinds the Beholder, and inspires Varis by pointing out said blindness. Nice. The flaming sphere moves towards Knob and he takes damage Varis is currently charmed, moving towards the altar, and puts down his bow. The cone of antimagic moves, and is hitting no one. The beholder throws an enervation ray at Knob who gets hit roughly by necrotic damage. 3 new rays! Fear Ray - at Knob Death Ray - at Varis Sleep Ray - at Varis The blind beholder sends out rays. Randall doesn't get targeted yet. The fear ray doesn't affect Knob. The sleep ray gets sent to Varis, who can't be put to sleep by magic. The death ray, lowers slightly and glows at Varis and he tries to get out of the way. Varis tumbles out of the way, somersaulting forward and is able to get out of the way just in time. But unfortunately he left his bow behind him, and it's left disintegrated into dust, a magical bowstring left in the ray's wake. And his charm is broken Knob heads to the beholder and attacks him straight on with the warhammer, with both hitting! TURN 3 OVER TURN 4 begins Randall holds his action until Knob can get to him and inspires Knob The beholder takes a legendary action and uses a slowing ray at Knob, who fails. The flaming sphere hits Randall The beholder throws the petrification ray at Varis, who again dodges out of the way, narrowly missing the stonework behind him Varis, without a bow, throws a chain lightning at the beholder who fails! Scorch marks form on the beast, but it's not quite enough Varis heads up to the Beholder and bursts hits with his sword. The beholder gets another charm ray at Varis, who saves. 3 more rays! Charm ray at knob, successfully saves. Sleep ray at Varis, but it doesn't work. Death ray at knob, so he tries to dodge the ray. And successfully gets out of it. But all his non-magical items are disintegrated, naked as the day he was born. Knob tries to attack the beholder, wailing through the air. He casts branding spite beforehand and using the inspiration dice, but doesn't quite do enough damage. END OF ROUND 3 Narlax The Dreamer disappears into a portal, with the ancient symbol of the magister's tournament Trial 5 has completed. Outside in the Valley of the Anchors Team Anemone came out first! And Team Lorelei is out already. Shockingly Narlax the Dreamer is cradled in the tree. Team Sprocket comes out next. Team Ashara exits as well Followed by Team Hayden with some lower halves of human beings Finally, Team Randall appears. It's announced that Randall has been eliminated. We'll meet at the tomb of the last curator at first light. All participants are required to attend with two chosen individuals/witnesses. Sprocket talked to Lorelei about their attack. The blue alien attacked the beholder with one hit and then disappeared. So we might want to go investigate that. Rest of contestants in the running - ashara, hayden, lorelei, anemone and sprocket. Of note, Hayden's a baddie. We don't know much about Ashara. We should try to figure out more. Randall looks out and sees the arrival of a man playing a box, 3string lute. With white skin. He can almost see the tail of spectrality? Floating about 3 inches in the air. Brigit sees a fox. THE ZODIAC. Fen and Sprocket are going to chill Randall and Brigit head towards the newcomer Varis is going to go find a new bow. Manitou! They need to borrow the rod from Cloyma. To bestow the power of the Alabaster Seat. New lore - 4 seats tied to the 4 types of magic. Natural, divine, arcane and the beyond. Each type of magic is tied to a seat. Each type of magic is tied to a zodiac. Brigit gets Manitou's form! And turns into a Star Fox Each zodiac is around whenever, but they are strongest during their respective month. Chill sesh Super chill. Super tight. Trixie makes a point not to stand next to sprocket. Bow Hunting He heads over to a band of warriors in search of a bow to borrow/buy/steal. Orso has a bow, offers to sell it. He's a quartermaster for a recently marooned ship, The Feyturtle. Maybe he can head over to The Distillery to check out our brewery. Varis pays full price for the bow, 200 gp. It looks sturdy enough and will do good for the time being. They're not part of the qualsahar's, so they don't get the same perks. But there's plenty of work at the distillery if they're looking for work. Now what? Fen pulls out his name card and calls out for Yuri Oh boy. A pool of water opens up underneath his feet and Yuri and Garth appear before him. He knows both Nyx and Xyn. Seems like Nyx has been causing problems in the summer court. Since he's been invited, he wants gifts. And Fen wants Yuri to take us there and back again. Fen pulls out his unbreakable arrow. But, doesn't specify a time. So he needs something else to bargain. Something magical or special The fully matured cask of beer from last night Randall sells it. The Premiere alcohol creator on this continent. Garth apparently loves beer. If he agrees, we'll even name a special beer after Garth from a plant in the feywilde. Garth takes the ponykeg and consumes it, holding it as a pocket of himself. We end up somehow explaining what 12 hours is on this plane, we need to be back less than 16 hours from now. TO THE FEYWILDE! Hugging Garth. Varis. Gets enveloped by the gelatinous mass and slowly disintegrates. Fen, Mehen, Brigit, Sprocket tells Lilith where he's going. And she warns him not to be late. Sprocket hugs Garth's leg. Randall. In the feywilde, trees surround us as we look around in summer night. Varis feels autumn, Fen is drawn towards Spring, each being drawn to their perspective areas. Mehen lands on Fen's shoulder and points towards the twisted meadow. Image of a wolf returning to the den, automatic response to going home. Brigit feels pulled in every direction. Most natural places she's been before. The stag and doe are pulling her towards autumn more. Randall and Sprocket arrive. Sprocket is surrounded by the underbrush. It's a little difficult to get through. He hops on the broom. Randall prestigitates the moisture off of his body. Fen looks around and leads us towards the edge of the forest clearing, being pulled by the wood too much. Brigit is super disoriented, like when you get off a roller coaster and the ground doesn't feel solid beneath you. Varis is able to tell the difference, he can start following Nyx's trail north. Up north, we come across a clearing There's a crazy bright sunlight in front of us. All the summer sunlight is saved up. And there's fields of flowers all around us. And a centaur approaches us, asking if we're here for the tea party. Her coronation is upon us. Her crown is the diadem She's supposed to make a wish. Oh no. He smells Varis and smells autumn. Varis bows deeply and says he'll behave as a guest. And his bow disappears. But he doesn't have the same qualms about Fen We're led in a field, all surrounded by insect wings Sprocket notices the pixies surrounding us, leading us into the court. Their yellow light has changed to a deep purple, red and blue to push us forward. A spring, autumn and winter emissary. Who's winter? The house of blood voting! A herd of horse like creatures, unicorns, centaurs, etc. Better known as…. colts, contributions, and coagulation in the colosseum caldera The horns are jagged like lightning bolts The festival of fire and tea is upon us Did we bring an offering for the festival We look out for Nyx - there's a white light. Varis recognizes the summer flowers Nyx disappeared into. They're asking for blood as a tribute offering from Varis specifically, as is tradition. Varis pulls out a gold coin, cuts his palm, coats one side in blood and flings it at whoever is shouting at him. But magnetically, it gets into the red-capped man hand. Deemed an adequate sacrifice. Spring's sacrifice has already begun. Everyone else, enjoy your tea. Brigit checks that we might want to drink the tea Randall drinks it. It's like wine, very sweet summer, ambrosia honey. It actually heals him. He feels warm and like nothing could make him cold. And he asks some pixies for the tea - 6 answer in a conjunction - connect morning dew, leave to dry, salt,  light on fire, 3 days in a row. Varis drinks the tea, it's so sweet, it tastes horrible to him, he can't get the taste out of his mouth and just hates it. The pixie's say we have to drink it to talk to Julianna Apparently the recent awakening of winter has caused some confusion in the feywilde. But the return of the summer lady means that we'll be able to solve some problems The dragons have been dealt with? Fen drinks. The centaur clamps down on his shoulder, one question deserves another. Does your friend not know he's the emissary of winter? SPROCKET If he wishes to be discreet, he can provide the blood in private. Or can have it extracted in a meeting with a queen. Fen asks Sprocket about his connection to the rabbit zodiac, which apparently makes him an emissary to winter. They can discreetly make this okay. Looking around, Sprocket doesn't see anything amiss, but Fen senses that we're probably being watched by every eye in the place. Save some hooded ones. We're sacrificing blood. And voting on whether or not we're going to war with winter. Grumbleson has awakened. We have to go to war and put him back to sleep. We've never met an emissary of winter. Ever. They've been finding them and they all have seemed to have fled the feywilde. Fen thinks this is an annual occurrence. The volume of blood given is the magnitude of the vote. Off in the distance, Randall sees two hooded figures coming towards us. One is floating. He recognizes one, a dragonborn, a really really big one, making his way towards Sprocket. Randall recognizes him from the Harvest Festival His breath  feels cool as he breathes on Randall Tumnus Driftwalker. Leader of the winter curators. He calls forth anyone who wishes to die first, not wanting to wait for the voting to finish. He feels immortal, they won't be able to spill his blood the first day of winter. Varis looks and sees Camille, she says she has to fix this. The fire genasi that escaped windhaven. She offers a needle to Sprocket, tells him to just vote a little bit. One small drop. Sprocket heads toward the basin, to vote in front of everyone. Varis makes a reach for Sprocket, spilling the thimble drink all over him, pulling him back, having him remember that this should be a secret thing. Steps back. Gets more advice from Camille. He takes a scrap of parchment out, puts one drop of blood onto it and Glen takes the paper. A bonfire is lit and one half of the quorum begins celebrating. Tumnus begins cleaving red hats in half on the other side. A hush falls across the crowd as Nyx walks up, addresses the crowd. The vote has been cast, it is time for the tea party. Nyx is wearing the diadem still. We can't look at the tea party because we're all staring at Juliana. Everyone she goes, wildflowers grow. She welcomes us, asks who will tell our plea. Looking back, the way behind us has been shut by two unicorns. Fen speaks up, we come for the diadem, The Raven Queen has sent us to retrieve the diadem so as to prevent Gard'daemon. Why should she listen to fate when she can make her own? Nyx would wish for a chance to undo all of the wrong Reynaldine has wrought. She's the knight of the flowered field. Fen explains that if you undo the wrongs, we'll never get to the rights. That by undoing what Reynaldine did, might give Vecna the upper hand. Why shouldn’t she be able to undo the things she did while she was under duress. And out of the tea party emerges a Tabaxi. She turns to the emissaries, Sprocket and Varis, do they have a problem with Nyx's wish, her wish. Varis thinks the sentiment is good, but is wary of the repercussions. And that it might turn bad on Nyx. The Tabaxi says that she should listen to the Raven Queen. Fate can't be stopped. Sprocket wonders if they could postpone? Varis points out the timing is suspect, since there's no alabaster seat right now. Without the seat, this could be disastrous. Since it's before the 6th trial, no contestant can die before tomorrow. Reynardine can't die until tomorrow. A shockwave of power runs through, the autumn power runs through Varis, then Randall. An arcane upside-down city is attached to the sky, and the tallest of three towers at the bottom. It's at the shape of a zenith. A pyramid of a pyramid. A zenith has opened. The Harvest zenith has been opened.

**Author**: Brigit

TLDR The tournament finished up - Randall and Sprocket were brought back to finish up the trial. Of note - the non-combatants were at the tomb of the last curator, but disappeared and no one checked where The 8th trial was a combat one again and sprocket threw Ashara under the bus The 9th was a show of cool And finally the 10th was a show of endurance. Anemone was crowned the alabaster seat Brigit found a ritual of rebirth in Eden with the Children of the Forest/Progenitors and now Brigit is one of them! Brigit found Finkle at the end and tried to kill him, but he narrowly escaped and she followed last second to the House of the Life and Death. To the House of Life and Death Wayy beneath Argos and there's tons of pestilence here causing all sorts of madness - and what you saw with Finkle. Parthenon-style area. Brigit jumps through as fire and sees a clear sign of battle around her. There are signs of the artificer's guild and signs of Argosian Finkle is headed towards a large carved door Brigit drops fire elemental and casts pass without a trace Hides Finkle knocks on the door in an easy pattern, slides in and the door closes Brigit goes to listen - Finkle thinks she's here, lock the door The walls are sandstone and covered with dragon marks… so it might be time to leave. Time to come up with a plan The rest of the party What's next for the new platinum seats? Two bards come up to talk to Randall The leader of the maroon cloaks - wants to hear about the rest of his travels, learn about his history An emerald cloak - telling Randall he's short, but we can fix that Ash cloak - elderly tortoise, interested in everything that led him here Etc etc Randall decides to schedule some office hours to get information about them. And he assigns all of them homework In stark contrast, Sprocket's moment is a quiet moment with Lilith. Who is immensely relieved, and looks forward to what he does with the position. She informs him to pay very close attention to his dreams from now on. She warns him that he's going to be very very busy. Her last vision has her interested in Conqui's Abbey. She's going to take Gideon to Shadowfell To the rangers They're scanning the crowd for anything suspicious Fen recognizes Jin Ling - and invites the crowd to Monethern as a place of safety and stability A number of wizards perk up and pay attention - all of anemone's changes and the fact that umbara populai isn't the alabaster seat. ~20% of people are probably leaving. And it's not all wizards - a fair number of Orchid cloaks are leaving. Varis notices some eager Qualasha'har people all talking to Anemone about reuniting teleportation networks. Anemone looks exhausted and is trying to make these decisions carefully. We have to vet the portals carefully - there's so many of them that we don't even know where some of them go. PORTAL-BAITED Varis moves in and pulls Anemone away and offers help to check out the portals, especially the hidden one. She glances quickly at Sprocket and then tells Varis exactly where to look - 3rd floor, Fen's going to be needed. Anemone makes to go away, but first tells the conjurer's wizards that Randall needs keys to his new tower. And then asks Sprocket to join her in 6 hours. Knob pushes forward to speak to Randall, and asks where he wants his workshop now - his new tower! And he'll make a teleportation circle on the boat to get there. Also of note, Scamp is okay, but he does have a new voice chip. And his eyes seem to be turning to remember information he might have forgotten. Varis also gives back the bow that he borrowed, showing off the bow he got from Reynardine Now what? Sprocket asks after Brigit! No one's heard after her in a while… Time to message: Are you safe, where are you. Tell us what we can do to help. Also anemone won tournament. Randall and I got promotions. Hollah. To Brigit: Semi-safe. In death dungeon below argos, found crazy finkle. Gonna try to get more info and sneak out. Meet you at home maybe? She seems okay. We'll meet her at home I guess? Fen's got some errands to run in Dawnpass, so he'll meet us back home in a few days. A yanti wants him to meet him outside of Dawnpass for a meeting. They setup a wellness check and maybe once they talk to Brigit they can tree stride or something And Fen's going to try to get a ride on the Qualsha'har ship/teleportation circle - all good. Additionally, Fen tells Trixie about Mahen and how she's not the same. And asks Trixie if she can watch over Mahen while he's running the errand. Fen also talks to Oswald And! That Stalwart setup a village and now goes by Stalwart Goldentooth. So Fen's going to head straight there. And they walk up to the ship and teleport away Also! We're in a magical city, so it's time to go shopping. But first, back with Brigit The door opens up and it's part of a much larger stone doorway. Also, there seems to be a separate room, with rune work that have been completely worn away. There is a monster peering through the doorway at Brigit. So seeing this thing and not wanting to die again, Brigit turns earth There's a sound of something/someone running… Brigit goes to intercept - a green cloaked figure, Kreena. She knows Fen! She was tracking the serpent zodiac but was led here? She came from the distillery - it's been attacked by a thing raining zombies. Dallor maybe? Something seems to have called it and it walked across the sea. We have to go back home and get the rest of the part. Kreena seems to think something is wrong with Mahen and she has to find Fen. Time to rest. Time to get back The quickest way back is through the portal down below, but that's not an option. So Brigit is going to rest and then we'll make our way over with her elemental form. Back to the shoppers. Sprocket and Randall are heading to the wandwrights Varis is heading to an alchemist to get a shitton of potions Sprocket heads to the center of town's wandwright Wherdo's wonderous quendesorian (?) Sprocket throws around his new title, to get some much deserved respect. He also holds up a wand of secrets (he's not sure what it is) - it's a wand that allows you to breathe underwater for an hour. He also pulls out an array of 6 wands. Sprocket points out two - the one that glows isn't as powerful, but it can detect when undead are nearby. The girthy totem pole one keeps track of different golems its seen (12), and you can locate them again, and it's the wand of the war mage. There's also a wand of magic missile, which Sprocket picks up. Varis and Sprocket head to the Apothecary! And are greeted by a dude with huge coke-bottle glasses on. He asks for a color preference? Varis asks for red, but that's boring. There's an emerald, cloudy serum with what looks to be movement in it and a yellow elixir with holy symbols etched into it. His invention (the cloudy) is square so it doesn't roll away from you when you need it Dude has so many potions. And is so cagey. These are all "healing" potions, but who knows what they actually do. Sprocket again throws around his title and the dude magically changes to make himself much more presentable. He's got 4 greater and one (ominous) superior, but he could also make another 6 healing potions (or 12 lesser)… 12000 gold, 5000 silver. Varis buys 2 superior, 2 greater and 12 regular. Randall heads to the Bard college to see if they can point him in the right direction. The shops are kind of in a farmer's market manner here - there's a spirit of competition In the ash bard's area - there's tons and tons of bunk beds, some connected together with small thread. He asks to get pointed in the right direction - there's a wand of polymorph, wand of binding. And he can hire some help anywhere where there are bards - for glory! But mercenaries can be found at the docks. Randall heads up to the stage to tell his story and try to get some bards to join the cause - to help maintain peace And everyone is just enthralled by him. Of note: Rengar Amore' - dwarven male, young, fedora scarlet cloaked bard that wants more Trixie in the story. Elven woman, middle-aged, not flashy but looks well-traveled, emerald cloak with a rapier on hip, lute across back. She's terrified of the beholder, but wants to know someone who's beaten a beholder. A teenage-halfing, maroon cloak, ska musician, high energy who plays horn and just keeps asking question after question and interjects and interrupts wither her thoughts. Fluted, amythyst elven bard is interested in the daring tales that don't all end in success. But it's interesting to commit the story and put her own specific spin on it, so that she can share it with other people. Randall's going to send her the full transcript. And a mustard cloaked bard, male gnome with a uke, drunkard and wants to be introduced to the ape. He's a cryptologist! And Randall gets the wand of binding, at the Unicorn's Tale And! He buys a round of (The Distillery) ale for all the contestants ^ Fen stands around stunned. But eventually heads into the Apothecary after seeing Varis and Sprocket exit the shop. He pulls out the big drought one, unless he can wait. You're not supposed to drink it, you're supposed to break the glass, stick your head in and breathe! So instead he takes the slippery potion. Fen then heads to the trader There's a bunch of women here with their hands stuck together with a coin between them - the Fox's artifact's and relic's emporium. She has some boots of elven kind - very light footed. There's also the candle of the deep? Grabbed both. Fen goes back to the apothecary to pay him and it seems like he's somewhat blind. Back to Brigit! We share stories - brigit tells about her recent portal travel and she tells about her psedodragon Follows directions 8 portals There's another one, but it's deeper. Where Brigit heard the snoring coming from. We can go back up and fly after another short rest Or we can sneak in and use the transformation as an escape hatch There's the portal, but near it, there's 8 doors and when Brigit comes near, she hears a child's cry. Knowing that children of the forest have been lured here, we have to check. Before we make it there, there are 2 armored skeleton wights. Brigit successfully charms them and they don't move. We head to one door and successfully unlock it with the guards keys. The children are covered with wax and we can't get it off, but we can move them. Kreena and I have two each and we're able to get all four out, but we're encumbered and no longer sneaky. It doesn't look like there's anyone in the other doors, but we really don't have much time to check. It's time to leave. Back to the rest of the party Share the spoils, chilling at the bar. Fen shows up and gives them the candle of the deep to take home for him. The group experiments with it by putting it into a mug of beer. It boils. And weirdly of note, the candle doesn't get the fish bowl warm, the water specifically - water of fire resistance And with that, Fen's off, taking Varis' offered healing potion. Hanging out with the party is the drunkard mustard cloak, he's super enjoying the distilleries' wares, and is successfully entertaining the party. Slosh Quibbledust. To the docks! Darla! And the thornberry wilde lower the ladder down to Fen. He apparently went to the sands seas and just chose a direction to walk in. The portal room has gotten an upgrade! Its become a decent library and scholar area. Trixie hands Fen a red envelope to give to Sprocket and a white envelope to give to Randall. To goldentooth! Outside of dawnpass. Tiberius appears! Just. So. Happy. To see Fen! He asks after the party - Brigit seems to be in danger, but Tiberious isn't sure why or where or how. It doesn't quite make sense why the yanti wanted to meet here. But we should be prepared for something to happen within the next 24 hours. There is a yanti child here - Sandra. She's been here since almost the beginning. Fen heads to the orphanage and runs into Stalwart's bear hug. Everything was too much, and Stalwart just wanted to put some good into the world. Stalwart offers to buy our beer, but it's a little weird because they're children. Anyway Fen chats with Sandra. She knows that he was supposed to bring the girl! Also, Fen has never seen a yanti child before, so this seems suspicious. They need Kareena, the girl, the one he was hiding. Fen says that he doesn't care about them and he's going to come after all of their clan. He tells them they're going to regret taking his connection to Mahen from him He tells her to go back to her clan with this message. And then leaves. 16 more hours before… The magister's circle The appointment with Anemone, and the third floor (not) The diviner's coven is spread then and we have to do some recruitment. She asks about Fen - he went to deal with an urgent matter. Maybe we can handle to portals when he returns tomorrow. She wants to speak with them about something, but they have to pose it as a question. Not about the past or future, but the present. When she tries to speak, the amythyst shatters a little, but subsides. Varis - what can we do to avert gard'daemon? That's a future question. Varis - How the keep and his people? Everyone's fighting. There are walls of zombies building up. There are a number of people being ferried into the grove my large eagles. Mac is shell shocked that vodram found him here. It's surviving. Sprocket - where would my friends and I be of the most use to you in this moment? NICE An image of young evan, carrying the heart of the faith (blue). He's in a room, but in one of the nautilus shell ships. In the sea near the serpent, there's a small collection of islands. Randall - how can I best prepare for what's there? There's a reunion we're going to need friends and allies for. You need Fen and the bow he wants. Prepare with your friends. Sprocket pulls out the black garment - the robe of the archmagi. But it can only be used by someone of an evil alignment. Could she refashion it? There are 3, so the other one would shift. Sprocket should meditate on the neutral robe in his dreams. We need all the help we can get. Especially tieflings. 3rd floor! Oswald is here, messing with the other half of the portal, trying to figure out where it's heading. It's headed to Argos. He's about to close it, when Brigit and Kreena are able to jump through. They close the portal, Brigit quickly explains who the children are and Oswald is able to get the wax off of their faces. Lots of loose ends to tie up. Until next time!

### Key Plot Points
- Brigit found a ritual of rebirth in Eden with the Children of the Forest/Progenitors and now Brigit is one of them!
- Brigit found Finkle at the end and tried to kill him, but he narrowly escaped and she followed last second to the House of the Life and Death.
- To the House of Life and Death
- Brigit jumps through as fire and sees a clear sign of battle around her.
- Finkle is headed towards a large carved door
- In stark contrast, Sprocket's moment is a quiet moment with Lilith. Who is immensely relieved, and looks forward to what he does with the position.
- She warns him that he's going to be very very busy.
- PORTAL-BAITED Varis moves in and pulls Anemone away and offers help to check out the portals, especially the hidden one.
- Knob pushes forward to speak to Randall, and asks where he wants his workshop now - his new tower! And he'll make a teleportation circle on the boat to get there.
- Semi-safe. In death dungeon below argos, found crazy finkle. Gonna try to get more info and sneak out. Meet you at home maybe?

**Author**: Brigit
**Party Track**: Tieflings

### What Happened

TLDR 3 different plots Brigit was trying desperately not to die again, and clawed out of the temple.
