Meaning that 7/12 of the children are saved. Fen also separated from the group to deal with a mysterious contact in the Goldentooth village that Stalwart Little had founded. Fen found something unexpected, a yanti child. But she seemed older than she appeared. There was also a series of conversations with Varis, sprocket, randall and anemone. Anemone needed Fen and Varis at the portal room at a certain time. And then the three also needed to go meet her in her office. The trio also bought a lot of stuff in the magister's circle. They were able to ask the Alabaster's Seat a question and get a true answer about the present. 3 things: How is the distillery - bad. We're being attacked by lots of undead creatures. Where can we do the most good? An island in the middle of the serpent sea, to find Evan. It seems like he's exploring a spaceship How can we best prepare? Gather your friends, specifically Fen, and his bow, Fang. What's Fen up to? First, he warns Stalwart to watch out for Sandra. He can take the portal back, but it closes at sundown, like 8 hours away. He also checks in with Tiberious - who figures out that something is weird. But he tells Fen to come back soon, with everyone else. Tiberious believes this place will be of great importance in the future. And Tiberious is aware that Sandra is weird is as well, as a goblin. He might not be aware she's a yanti. And then Fen heads back to the qualshahar ship. Back in the Magister's Circle Oswald is melting candles off of the children of the forest. Brigit gives him the lowdown about Finkle - how he went crazy, has the heart of the wilde embedded in his eye. How there was a dragon or something down there. And that there's an underground city beneath Argos. 4 more children - a redheaded girl with emerald eyes and bird feather tattoos. And another girl that appears to be made of air or clouds. A brown haired, heterochromic boy with a scar running down his face/neck. And a red haired girl with feathers in her hair, emanating a yellow light. Bark, breeze, lumen and clora. The locket, demenor. Varis takes a look at the portal, but notices Karina first who is covering her heart. It's going to take a few days for the portal to completely close. Oswald is in the running for the conjuration platinum seat, so for now he can be in charge of these portals. So they setup some guards. He pulls out a pink crystal and uses it as a walkie. Sprocket is going to use Oswald's spell to fade into the image of the not-portal. Randall also notices that there's a bard on the outside of the room, seeming to be waiting to talk, or eavesdropping or something. He has a letter for Randall. It's a letter from Ciren, the scarlet cloaked bard. Clora talks to Brigit. She feels 10 of the other children doing something. That might be the other's at the Grove, so we can get them there tomorrow. Back to Fen He's at the qualshahar portal, trying to find Sprocket's tower. And Trixie has changed the prompt to include 'that no good dirty rotten…' Fen steps out of the portal and steps into hell. The tower's top has been pushed off, and full sunlight is streaming in, and outside are the sounds of war. The beast out in the ocean, does appear to be slipping on something, this 700ft tall nightmare. It does appear to be clamoring up the underground cliff. And there are creatures that are wielding boulders and flames as a siege on the tower. Magister's Circle 3 wizards are running in with a big crash cart of magical materials. Trixie, a woman with a maori-style red and blue tattoo and a male gnoll all walk in. Oh the other way! Code blue! They take a scroll and wallpaper the portal. With Sprocket behind There's barely a shadow behind the wallpaper and Trixie slightly grins before enabling the wallpaper. Bones of the Earth. 12 dowels slam into the wall, holding the wallpaper in place. Another portal opens up that's face to face with the other portal. And the other portal goes to a bottom of a volcano. Trixie turns to Randall - a lot of people have been asking about the two doctors. And she wants him to try to take her out of it. Yay. Randall's going to try to perform a different version that can take Trixie out of it, yeah like she can even been killed off after a minute long monologue. The guards leave and as she walks out, Trixie tells Sprocket to step forward and he'll be fine. Sprocket dimension doors out of the conundrum. What's next? Varis wants to go home. Brigit needs to get these children home. So we're going home. Fen? We don't know. We're assuming he won't be there. We can try sending? And we need to sleep. So maybe Sprocket's office? To Sprocket's office! Someone stops Sprocket on the way. What's the password? Sprocket pokes around the ethereal plane, and there are 3 eyes at the front door - green, blue and red. Let pass the scarlet tunic brigade. New password set, and they'll come check with him tomorrow. And the next person automatically knows the password Into the office! The dome of his office is a map of Mardov - a globe rather! Sprocket finds a spot that calls to him, and then just walks across a water shelf. And we enter through the magister's circle on the globe. And of note, there's other things on the map that diviner's have noticed on the map All with different markers on them 1-10. Serpent Sea, vag mahar, macchu and back home at the distillery. But only Sprocket sees them. Maybe going home isn't the best idea. The world is falling apart. Varis and Brigit need to go home. But maybe we can get Evan to come to us? At the least, we can evacuate the Distillery So let's actually send some messages Every grid of Sprocket's office seems to have a different function - kind of in a cubicle format. Bookcases, crystals, candles and a (secret) ladder heading up to the third floor. And a small apartment with a king-sized human bed, and a wine rack of spell scrolls. The crystal ball is attuned to the valley of anchors. Sprocket makes the chime of a bell with minor illusion. 30 minutes later, a large glob of dirt gets thrown up and looking down there are 7 apprentice wizards trying to help. They can all do messages yay. To Fen: (who has been at the tower for 30 minutes) Actually, to Fen. There are some people being air-lifted out of the keep via giant eagles. And a fairly constant barrage of arrows flying down at the zombies. Fen's going to try to join up with the army in the Grove. Fen attempts to dive underneath, and swim through the bay and into the grove. There's a splash behind him as the zombies see him underneath the bridge. He keeps swimming He outpaces the zombies behind him, but he's still swimming along. More are falling into the water, and heading to a small dock in Claudere keep. They're going to storm the castle via the backdoor. Fen rises up and then shoots a lightning arrow at the first pack of zombies, shooting off to hit mulitple groups Then, some of the zombies come for him Underneath the water are ghosts of people, ghosts of our former friends, who were originally at the keep. He keeps trying to get to the grove, he's at the shoreline. The spectre's follow him, but can't quite keep up. He heads up to where the horde is to try to lead them into the grove. They break out into a sprint towards Fen, who runs into the grove. A massive T Rex - 4 actually, bust through the trees to chomp up the zombies 2 beavers hop up and start talking to Fen. You're not supposed to be out there! You have to come with us. Fen refuses to go with them, and he asks for people to help distract the zombies. To the elves! Anabelle, the carver, slaps Fen, you said it was going to be safe here. She saw someone's soul leave their body and attack someone else. There's at least one battalion left in the keep, but most of the people are in the group. Mack is also in the forest. The zombies showed up, the centaur grabbed some people and took them back to claudare keep. But when someone dies, they come back as both a zombie and a spectre. The children made everyone feel okay. Most of the structures are on fire, but Fen's temple was in a spectral bind and a blue light came out of the fountain. Everyone is supppper mad at Fen and scared. And he has no answers. They've been under attack for a day and a half now. And the serpents left before this happened. They've been doing hit and runs all day. But they've still got it in them to do a few more Ring ring Hey its sprocket. Give your status. Rest of party at magisters circle. Three more important events. Thoughts? We all want to live, what do you want me to say? Keep under attack. Lots of zombies. Lots of dead people. When are you coming? And to evan: (first, between sprocket describing and randall illustrating a picture, they get the idea) Hi evan. This is sprocket from long ago. We're concerned about you. How urgently do you need help, we're in magister circle Abishai are outside. Id ont know how they found me but they found me. Looking to stars as a final solution. Good luck. Randall decides to head over that way, and meet up with him. Or at least try to. And we send over a message explaining that. If you travel by sea, be aware of the serpent. Storms, giants and more than a little bit of hell await you, good. So Randall's heading that way, but is first going to stop in Cloud City and grab someone. More things A scarlet tiefling walks forward - addresses the Pegasus knight. Freaking Cirin. He's coming with apparently. Cirin denotes out an annoyingly loud beat with his walk/tail. Randall walks a complicated electronic thing. Everyone around is confused and cirin is just in his own world. Randall confiscates all of Cirin's gear and then if he behaves, he can get it back. Lorelei congratulates Randall on his promotions, and explains where Goodbeing is. And to mess with Cirin, Randall casts dimension door on his bed to go into crew quarters. Back at the office Sprocket takes a look at the scroll spells. A lot of these can't be cast by wizards. Sprocket grabs three **perfect** scroll spells to be determined later Back to Fen He's trying to get to his temple He uses the gardeners to get there and at the right moment, panthers from Team Nature come in to attack the zombies And he makes it into his temple. There's someone that has been here. And also, the message to Karina is gone. Fen prays to the gods and a creature appears, an elven woman with green skin and light blue armor, Sidereal Vizier. Why is this being here? He is here to be harvested. And they are next. Every single zombie, ghoul and spectre disappates. Just leaving behind Dallor and resetting the clock in the distillery's life. The earth erupts beneath the zombies and serpents appear to swallow them whole. Dragging everything downward. And left behind where the Sidereal Vizier was, is an image of a burned dragon Fen sits, alone, and waits. Long rest and ship sailed. Randall aboard his ship Arya is on deck, producing a beautiful pirate ballad for the journey. Arya left when the sky fell, but she's interested in seeing how they've built back up. He's looking for Festerous back in the Storm Peak's. And Randall gives her the revision to the Star-crossed lovers, as requested by Trixie. She's totally into it. Job well done. Down to Goodbeing Arya follows for awhile, but it seems like Goodbeing turns her away at the last second (jedi mind trick) Goodbeing was told to stay below deck, but with Randall's permission, transforms into a conehead humanoid, and it's a realllly bad imitation of a human. So instead, Randall tries to illustrate what he might want to look like. Snapchat filter! It's great, it's beautiful. The seas between are plagued with serpents. The serpent specifically. We should maybe go far around him. Or we could fly? There are 3 entities that have forced their way in, and one with a key that he lost. So who knows how many people we're going to need. What should Goodbeing's new name be? Bean. Magister's Circle Varis, Sprocket and Brigit fall asleep in Sprocket's bed. And Sprocket dreams Of Grey He's taller than before, rolls out of bed and he feels like an otherwordly being. He sees a being in the mirror without a nose and white eyes, parchment skin. Sprocket-creature changes to a blue dragon. Blues and whites, greys and blacks fall out of the closet. Heading outside to other dragons, one silver and one white, 'he who speaks for righteousness, welcome to the land of mortals. What can we do for you?' Outside is a holy place, a fountain with silver water, a statue of a silver scaled dragon. The left hand of justice also walks out with the sprocket-creature. And, this creature is also a fraud. In the statue of Bahumut, the space where a large blue gem should be stands empty. Also, a red-headed man walks up and greets our Sprocket-creature. Vickrum? Perhaps? 'Show me to the battle?' ## Chapter 85T -- A Tale of Two Storms'Burps and Farts
**Author**: Brigit
**Party Track**: Tieflings

### What Happened

TLDR Two main stories: Fen v. rest of the party. Fen was in Goldentooth village, spoke with Tiberious about Sandra, but portal-ed back to The Distillery and began the fight against the zombie invaders. Whenever one of the defenders dies, they become a ghost. Then he made it through Faughart to his own temple, and summoned a member of the Feywilde who was able to drop a motherfucking bomb and reset the clock on this battle. All the zombies and undead disappeared except for the big baddy, Dallor Dallor Bill. In the magister's circle, Trixie took care of the portal with a dope-ass crash cart. We were introduced to the Scarlet cloak of Bard's, who joins Randall on his journey. We went to Sprocket's office and got some more information, and some goodies. We were also able to send all the messages - to Fen and Evan. Note: the same people who almost killed us are after Evan. Finally, Sprocket had his first vision about the right and left hands of justice within Conquis Abbey. Randall headed to Evan, with the rest of the party heading to the distillery, hopefully to meet up before too long. Additionally, Reynardine is coming to help for Varis, at least he pointed to help with the interest of a portal. With Fen He's at his temple, among the ash of the dragon left behind from the fey. And it's like nothing ever seen before. A large scream comes from the docks as Dallor reaches the cliff of the sea wall - more and more zombies of various forms falling from his body. Fen heads to Claudare Keep, where there are tons of scared looking townsfolk - Iris, part of The Quiver's, comes to greet Fen. There are 10 fighters left - they decided to stay behind to protect the castle. And it seems like 7 of them have fevers Everyone else has either died or gone to forest. And people are now heading back to the castle from the forest. Mac reports ~400 able bodied people/2000 coming from the forest. The woods-witch and the centaur can send messages, but they're back in the forest. It should be around an hour until the next wave, but we'll try to get there as soon as possible. Fen waits to see what happens. Back in the magister's circle: Sprocket wakes up to see Varis standing in front of a portal. Is it safe? We can't take the children of the forest through there. Sprocket summons an intern to message him in a few minutes to determine whether Brigit can bring the kids with her this way. Varis and Sprocket head through the portal BIFROST Weirdly enough, Varis feels like he's in a swamp. They appear in a cove, face-to-face with some creatures. They're on a boat, and are not associated with the qualshahar. And there's a large snail on the bow, and Merlock's (swamp creatures apparently) Kuo-toa. They're fabled to exist, but no one knows if they actually do. Additionally there's a legend that a boot became a god after they worshipped it so much. INCOMING MESSAGE We're okay, we landed on a boat with fish people. Thanks for calling, tell brigit. And turns out, one of them speaks common. Nice. Or rather, they understand it. Geographically speaking, they think they're on the west coast, less than a day away from the distillery. Varis pulls out his bow, and says that he was sent by Reynardine. All goes quiet, and they all bow before Varis. And they begin taking the boat north, realizing that this boat doesn't have any sails, but is still moving. One of the fish tosses some slime towards Sprocket, he dodges and the slime goes overboard. The guy immediately jumps over and Sprocket throws a fishing net over. Brigit inspects the slime, and they begin to burn with sulfur and scorched earth. And takes in a vial of the slime. How is the boat moving? Two large giant octopi are moving the boat, with three large sharks Nice, let's help this out. Brigit makes some more octopi to help push the boat, cutting the time down. Back to sailing Randall runs into a storm The wind genasi on board is the only happy one, allowing the wind to play her bagpipe for her. To get around it, we can either go way north or way south, let's go north! During the sail, Randall goes around the boat to tidy some things up, take control of it, take stock of things. And he puts Lovelace to work, by mopping the deck. 2 sisters are practicing aerials, but are having some trouble in the storm. Randall helps secure them down by tying them to the boat. Knob appears to be cooking. Scamp is farting the smell to as an air detergent. Lorelei is practicing, and just wants to know if her father can join them. The cloud breaks, and the boat is able to land. Randall rides on his pegasus upwards, but knob comes to speak to him - there might be some eucalyptus and some gadgets he can pick up. And he's off! The centaur appears, and takes Fen back into Faughart. Cersei is talking to 3 wizened women, casting dice/bones on the ground. Message to Brigit - We've lost 1/3-1/2 the village, two or 3 hours before the next fortification and if they could come as soon as possible, that would be helpful. Hold up as much as you can, we'll be there soon. Any more info? The monster's in the sea, with an army of the undead, everyone's holding up in the keep. Sprocket goes to control the weather in It's warm, some wind, with an eerie fog. A rolling storm comes in overnight, blowing the zombies away from the shore. Fen stays on the castle wall all night long, dropping bodies. Additionally, some of the smarter zombies are climbing back onto Dallor Dallor Bill instead of trying to swim to shore. The night is fruitful, and The Quiver eventually just go to bed and leave Fen to it. To Randall The pegasus pushes through the cloud wall, the city has had some time to discover wood, now that they're on the ground. And heads to Thestrus' shop. Thestrus is stoked to see Randall again. What motivates him? His work. True, inspired, artistry. What if he could see a place with work beyond this world? Even beyond the Feywilde? He's in! Underneath the anvil, is a large iron backpack. This dude is ready to go. He's going to have to be pulled behind the ship, but that's okay. Dr. Grackle is back! Knob and Scamp found his shop and called over Randall. Dr. Grackle believes he can bring a lot to the table, as another artificer - a traveling workshop, apparently it can fly. It's a hot air balloon! Well, almost. The balloon gets stuck, and the building completely shatters outward. Grackle wants half of what Randall is paying Knob. Sure why not. Knob begins to work on a raft design from the torn out workshop. Next day at the Distillery The coastline comes into view The creature is huge, much larger than Sprocket's tower. Sprocket begins drumming up the weather again To Faughart There's damage all around, barely recognizable from when we left it last. Heading to where the children of the forest live Warn them about the portal, they're going to get a message to Cloyma They've been healing people as they've come in. Reincarnate, foresight, heal and raise the dead, spirits into bodies and huge restores. Iris went off a while ago to the Tides of the St. Ashbury's there were some humans associated with the undercruft? We've got to add it to the quest list. The animals have been great and Cersei is awesome at battle tactics - Brigit needs to go check in with her Sprig thinks that Dallor is really sick, there's something wrong with his heart and he can fix it if he gets close enough. We can do that. Claudere Keep The castle roars up with joy as Varis appears. Mack emerges out of the keep, they lost about 3/4 of the village/fighters. The spectre's were unexpected (different from Vodram), and that took out a lot of them. Iris has been amazing, and deserves a promotion To Sprocket He's gonna mount up on his broom, and make a mad dash to his tower, trying to stay off the coastline And, the Change Weather gets revved up Strong winds, light clouds To… Gale winds, out towards the ocean and lower the precipitation to clear. Sprocket touches a very specific point on the ground and casts Forbiddance, setting up a Beach of Normandy, using one of his spell scrolls, and a volcano begins to erupt from the earth However, casting time of 10 minutes Fen Minigame! There are some zombies coming towards Sprocket. He sends out a regiment to go protect him and starts picking off zombies around Sprocket Fen is able to pick off 2 of them. Mac takes The Quiver out to protect Sprocket and Varis is going to talk to Kasim in the meantime. One of the smarter beasts, a wight, pull out a bow and aim at Sprocket, Fen casts a grasping vine arrow at him and he's restrained. And NAT 20, critical hit. Sprocket gets hit, but is still able to hold onto his concentration. The second shot misses, flying wide. Brigit and Cersei We're sending everyone out. She is not at all surprised that Brigit is turning into a dragon. Unfortunately, something that big isn't going to be easy to get close to, so Brigit's going to head up to the keep, and keep watch and when the creature is distracted/falls, she'll know it's time to take Sprig out. Cersei is ready to fight with everyone and everything. The monsters are all ready to go and it's going to be fun as hell. Varis and Kasim The monster is from Vodram, and Kasim has made a little figurine of it. It feels like home, but there's something else - a second heart within it. A fragment of Mardov itself is within him. This is what happens when you combine Mardov and Vodram. The power lures them, and they drop immediately down into the pit, away from the main battle. At the very least, it can take care of the zombies for now. There's enough vodram in there that if we take care of the mardov part, he can paralyze an arm or a leg. "Danger is a given today" Sprocket and Fen Fen takes another shot at the zombies attacking Sprocket, getting another, but there's another group coming at Sprocket. MASS WARFARE Sprocket spell completes The wind begins to shift The sun emerges and the battle begins Skeletons move forward, get hit by the barricade. Fen hits at an enemy, attacks Sprocket How we're going to play it - every player attack Dallor, every unit attacks each other. Fen attacks Dallor, shooting him with his bow. Fen and Varis and Brigit confer, the elves are going to take a ride on Brigit-dragon when the time comes. Brigit and Sprig make their way down the mountain, heading closer to Dallor The Nightwalker/Dallor, tries to move forward but can't, he screams forward a stream of ghosts and banshee's at Mac's platoon, a Finger of Doom spell. He also throws some rocks at the quivers, hitting them. Sprocket uses another spell scroll - EARTHQUAKE Every corporeal creature gets knocked down by 2. Dallor gets hit bad and just, deals with it. The quake also cause a bit of a morale check for the zombie units Back to the units! Karina heads out, shoots at one of the skeleton boys. The eavallan fighters move in The villagers retreat and defend the castle The quivers move up and take pot shots The banshee's engage Mac's party, but it doesn't hit Frell, cersei and trex's move forward The quiver recruits take more pot shots Big cats move forward The bodaks come out of the water The kuo-toa move in to defend The quivers are engaged and go to attack the banshee's And they hit and get diminished! The wights move forward barely The zombies move forward barely The wind kicks in The skeletons begin moving forward More zombies are created Back to the players! Fen cures some wounds of the quiver recruits Brigit throws moonbeam at Dallor, gets a bit of damage Varis casts Guardian of Nature, ground all around us is difficult terrain, and then gets super advantage at Dallor Nightwalker/Dallor, shoots at quiver recruits Sprocket hurls two fireballs - a spell and the tower. Focus in on Randall He gets about a 2.5 days journey. Around Bynar, the water seems to boil beneath the ways, and an iceberg/glacier is floating about the water Deep beneath the waves, Randall sees a writhing figure with a light that ignites and goes out. He tracks it and can see it move beneath the waves. Suddenly, a rush of bubbles happens and a building appears from beneath the waves, from an angular fish. The building has ancient elven runes on it. Nothing that Randall has ever seen before. The ship holds, Randall holds, but some of the darlight children fall off, and are held on by the rope. Beren Rather falls off and Thestrus attempts to grab him, and just manages to grab him. The bright light appears and Randall can finally see the large toothy maw of a Leviathan - the elemental evil of water. The story Liza told him comes back, how she was stuck on a raft with her brother. This thing can eat your whole ship and send you to another plane of existence. To be continued…

### Key Plot Points
- Kuo-toa. They're fabled to exist, but no one knows if they actually do. Additionally there's a legend that a boot became a god after they worshipped it so much.
- INCOMING MESSAGE
- One of the fish tosses some slime towards Sprocket, he dodges and the slime goes overboard. The guy immediately jumps over and Sprocket throws a fishing net over.
- Randall rides on his pegasus upwards, but knob comes to speak to him - there might be some eucalyptus and some gadgets he can pick up. And he's off!
- The monster's in the sea, with an army of the undead, everyone's holding up in the keep.
- Sprocket goes to control the weather in It's warm, some wind, with an eerie fog.
- Fen stays on the castle wall all night long, dropping bodies.
- The pegasus pushes through the cloud wall, the city has had some time to discover wood, now that they're on the ground.
- It's a hot air balloon! Well, almost. The balloon gets stuck, and the building completely shatters outward.
- Warn them about the portal, they're going to get a message to Cloyma

## Chapter 87T -- Ritual of Reincarnation 2
**Author**: Brigit
**Party Track**: Tieflings

### What Happened

TLDR/Slide show The distillery was under siege, by who? The yanti/ananta were there, but they weren't the real issue. Also of note, they wanted karina/varis, people who can jump between worlds However, two ghosts of the party were sent by a necromancer The craftmaster's helped us out, with a few other allies. Of note, the emerald veil arrived as a new ship, a spaceship. Randall and Thestrus were both swallowed by a whale. And also, evan and another automata (HAL) is on the spaceship. Nyx used the anchor of pestilence, the diadem to protect the harvest zenith and broker peace between spring, autumn, summer fey's to save the zenith Two places of attack - the beach (Dallor) and the forest (Tiny Rick) Freld fell during the battle, Sprig and Chlora were able to get within range of the crystal hearts and shattering them. The rest of the zombies were able to be picked off easily without their leaders. Also, Devin joined our group. We open at dawn the next day We're in the middle of faughart, and there's a shallow grave containing Freld that is covered in flowers, and everyone has flower crowns Freld has to die before living again. There's a procession of two lines of elves, and Brigit with the rest of the children of the forest, flowers are being thrown everywhere. Brigit covers her hands in oil and ointment, reaches through the grave and casts the spell. A group of gnolls growl and howl and come forward to dig through the dirt. The children of the forest also walk forwards with 6 other bodies to reincarnate. Back 12 hours Peace within Faughart. Brigit rolls in, drops her dragon form and checks on the children on the forst. They're okay, just tired. But also, they have to change forms. And they can save people, but they must also lose their forms. They want to be able to do good, and want to stick around for longer. What happens after the battle? Daina checks on her warforged creations, gets beamed up into the spaceship. HAL is micromanaging Dr. Grackle. And they're heading to the beach. Knob, scamp and Daina all notice HAL and the warforged is similar to what we've seen before, but also way beyond (technologically-wise) what they've seen before. The gem on HAL's belt glows when Daina touches HAL. He's resonating with her, somehow. And there is a very short list of people who could've created a creature such as this - craftsmanship-wise. There's a coat of arms, with a modification on it. A spear maybe? But she can't seem to access information about a 13th piece. HAL also warns her that it could be dangerous to continue this line of questioning. During this time, knob and scamp have been taking moss and plants out of the automaton and HAL is also changing them to move more fluid. Can HAL fix Daina? He swings a mallet hard onto Daina. Sprocket Goes to his tower, and finds an elven girl there - Sally. To Fen! Takes a short rest in his temple. Mahen has actually come back to the temple and made a nest in the rafters. Two elven fighters come up to the temple - Laura and Felborne Coalharth This is the temple of the serpent, but it's meant to honor all of the zodiacs in a way They're looking for work here. Varis In the keep, gets the rundown from Mac and Kasim Iris also comes into the room to join and discuss PTSD from the battle. Remembering the fallen, and remembering what we're fighting for. He hands her an ointment to help get rid of the smell of the undead and the bloody. (Mint and Lavender, beeswax). Mac and probably Sprocket will want to talk to the conjuring guild to rebuild. There was also a mention of a contract from Mr. Tux about beer, that we actually need to make. We don't know who was responsible - Veccna maybe? What are the next steps and where did these beasts come from? Dallor died in Vodram, deep underground in the tomb of the last curator. He was just left to rot in vodram, so was TR. They also took in some of the wandering allies into the keep - some of the mercenaries. First invasion, we lost at least 3000, a third of the town. Second invasion was still bad. Apparently some dwarves have been passing around a petition - only dwarves should live in the underdark. - Writ of Muradin Destiny Something about mineral rights Resurrection Lorelei comes and gives Brigit a vial of human parts for the ceremony. Randall/HAL's choice (^), Daina's choice, Dallor, TR, Dwarf brewmaster, Queill Stranafan (elf), Freld, Mia Before the night HAL starts working on making radiant light bombs from Nocturnous' knowledge Varis teaches and builds funeral pyres throughout the night, charred bones go into the ocean. And then gathers a list of everyone that died from this - the first work order, a memorial with names everywhere in Cladere Keep. Fen falls asleep in the temple, and at some point hears someone bringing a pack into the temple - his brother. He's going to stick around for some time. Karina also speaks to Fen - she just wants to sleep. And she'll also work to help fix Mahen. Fen's tired of fighting, tired of being brought into fights from gods. Brigit takes care of the forest - bringing bodies to Claudere keep and the pyres. And then she runs around as an elemental and checking on the forest. There's a meeting of the elves - they're disagreeing on whether this ritual should be done. The machu elves are not happy that the evallen elves let in finkle and endangered the children - we're going to attack him. The chosen elf is a follower of Evandra - goddess of cultivation and travelers Next day - The ritual Randall/HAL's choice (^), Daina's choice, Dallor, TR, Dwarf brewmaster, Queill Stranafan (elf), Freld, Mia The pallbearer's appear with the human figures or pieces of human figures Dallor - 21 - Hill dwarf Tiny Rick - 68 - Light foot halfling Daina - Dwereguar. A grey dwarf. Dwarf brewmaster - 85 - Lizardfolk, male Elf - 78 - Kenku, but then turns back into an elf, carrying the livery of the raven queen Mia - Hill dwarf Randall's vial - 79 - Kenku, NB The ritual The gnolls are biting and chewing at freld's dirt and the children place their objects into the same dirt, and complete the ritual. In daina's hand, back into her body, is the heart of the mountain. There is one other comfortable in their skin - Randall. Lorelei comes up and slaps Randall. Freld crawls out of the grave, looking at Sprocket - he did his best, and he's sorry He learned something - the elementals are moving, and they're converging on a mountain range in Monethern, in the mountain zenith. There's danger for her brother. Two more dwarves. Dallor passes out, but is faking it and Brigit flicks his nose. Dallor and TR remember: There was a storm and they were both there. Guineve and Grumpiere were there. They were imprisoned in the storm, a dark whirlpool of smoke and sludge. A face without the left eye, a skull with a red light pulled him out. He remembers the smell of the ocean and Sprig comes forward - it's like the ritual but backwards. It's not like coming back to life and starting over, but more like a weight of the past life maybe? Sprig isn't sure if Dallor, TR or Brigit was a good idea to bring back to life. TR said come to the forest, get the children, take the tower. Sally comes to the ritual with Sprocket and Cersei immediately jumped in. The forest zenith was already opened. Finkle took the heart of the wilde. There's something in the temple. Mia also steps in and tells Sally everything is going to be okay. Varis checks on Dallor and TR, their bodies have been desecrated. There's a fey presence around and there are celestial presence's from everyone raised up. Dallor and TR can stay within the distillery, but will be kept watch upon. Reynardine appears. Fen makes a deal - vizier's not here. What's his mantra if he took over the throne of mardov from the raven queen? All things serve fate, even death. => R would insert a concept of choice and choosing your own fate. We apparently should talk to some of the allies that were in the tomb of the first curator. And he calls down Evan - with the heart of the faith. There's a shield in bynar owned by the curator's, and it's possibly the anchor of pestilence. The 4 leaders take turns leading bynar. Tumnus Driftwalker. Sprocket also mentions that there was someone in his tower, took a map and took some spells. Fen and Randall have a word He doesn't seem very forthcoming. Like, did he achieve his goal? Not yet, but he will. What about the spectre? Or Lorelei seeing you die? He doesn't know. And would have to see it for himself. Better question - how do you kill a god? You defy the god in every way. Randall's going to head out to the magister's circle to take care of being the platinum seat. But it's a lie. Randall leaves. Future plans - let's stick around for a bit, mourn, rebuild and figure out the next step forward.

## Chapter 88 -- Distilling the Pieces
**Author**: Brigit
**Party Track**: Full Party

### What Happened

TLDR We had a ritual of reincarnation There was a coat of arms - viruvian man, with 13th spokes instead of 12 Sally. SALLY?!? Was found in Sprocket's tower Additionally, an intersex assassin infiltrated the tower and stole some tomes, Birt reported to us. And then they fled over the water. Varis dealt with the aftermath of the battle, and dealt with the bodies in the Vodram custom. Iris was promoted into the Countess of Claudere Keep (knighted in a way) And of big note, the attack happened on the first night of winter. Fen has become disillusioned and doesn't feel like he has control of his life. Of note, Karina, Lorgramar and Freld all said 'Lok' Tava' to Brigit, thus following a different type of religion Flashback - team nature went to the feywilde in chapter 51 and Mehen hatched within the feywilde and this is important. Additionally, Brigit got a lot of information about her locket here. More about the reincarnated, and how they live now. Of note: Kenku's have the ability to take on a different form and a different voice, which is we they were able to become their former forms. Quest reminders: Evan wants to keep the heart of the faith (stolen from conquis abbey) and the key to the winter's zenith safe from Rimefang, Still need the anchor of pestilence from tumnus in bynar. Sprocket made a promise to Gideon, taking a card to Hindersnap. (There's a raven's feather pulled from Sprocket's mind and placed in Mordred's spellbook) Fen wanted a meeting with the Yanti, and was to deliver Karina to them yesterday We have 8 children of the forest, but there are 12 total. And the heart of the progenitors is Grat'z. Scale was never heard from (another child). Radiant children - Argent and Regalia have also been found. Harvest Zenith has been opened and has been defended by Gabriel within the Autumn domain. There's also an onslaught of demons (led by Grat'z) attacking. And, the Anchor of Famin, Diadem of Dawn was left within the Winter's Sanctum with Grumblesin of the Winter court. Fang has been mentioned 3 times by different people to Fen, aka the Serpent, but no idea if Fen wants to contact Fang. Operation elven freedom - Argos is fucked, and we need to deal with Sally's uncle Jafar. (similar to Nocturnus). @ note - House of Life and Death was close to the ritual of reincarnation, close to what Brigit saw at the Toad Pool. Plans Sprocket - boat and hindersnap Brigit - talk to Sally, figure out the tower, and then life and death temple Varis - chill. Recoup and regenerate. Talk to karina. Other than that not sure, 6 fingers, maybe dealing with Mr. R and the eladrin impersonators. Maybe help out with Fang/Fen. HAL - fixing up robots, not sure about steps forward. Fen - contact Yuri, help Karina find Fang, but wants Mahen back. Might go back to the shadowfell hindersnap. Big party. Let's get drunk Stalwart ordered some beer, but we don't have any! So, let's talk to our new brewmaster - who can't currently taste. But he has a massive book of tastes and stuff to put in beer. HAL underclock's their CPU, but at some point feels a sending stone speaking from knob and scamp, discussing Daina's reincarnation. Scamp is very interested in how a mechanical thing could become a living thing again. Varis, Mac, Kasim and Iris all hang out together. Iris is dancing on the tables, enjoying life. But there's joy here too, after so much pain. We'll stick around for a little bit, hang out at the Distillery for a little bit of time. They discuss Dallor and TR, and how they were plucked from a place of souls and used by Vecna, turned into zombies, and sent to attack us. Mac wants to get to know them better. Fen stumbles into Karina, and explains that the Yanti were looking for her. But, they'll have to discuss it when sober. And she also wants to know more about his bow, gotten from some place in the Feywilde. But maybe he's a good guy now. And maybe with the bow, Karina can make Fen smile. Walking through the woods with the Lizardfolk - Sprocket, Brigit and Daina His previous employer, Z, the Mardov 6 fingered man. They were to select books from the tower and take it back to him. Daina says they were building a new tower. They were interested in our movements and how they knew to be at the Westrun fields. Katarina - of the postal service, couriers, headed back to Dawnpass for the secondary dead drop location. The emerald tavern, sundown tonight. Let's delegate. Lorelei! She'll go, but she wants to take the spaceship and the robots. A red headed lady will be at the emerald tavern. She has glasses in front of her (summer's rest), hal (amber ale), brigit (harvest day IPA), daina (ocean water), sprocket (water) Brigit talks to Mia They're alternating between their two glasses and talking to someone. We missed our chance, the last prophesies happened on the first day of summer. She points to an Aaracokra, and another human half-elf bard, Fovis, and Frozensong are the new woodswitches, and already said their prophesies yesterday. Heading to the aarocokra - Birt He won't tell me the information, but we could make a deal about the birtcoins. To Fovis. In birt's tower What was lost shall now be found; entombed in rock which once grew from the ground. A gift handed down through hands of four; only to be returned to he who lost it before

The power to break the mountains spine, frozen in the forest for all to find

The mantle of power, thou shalt that bear; the burden of war can still be prepared. War above, war below; fourth opened, never closed. The horn of winter, reclaimed from snow, the second harbinger from the past ago.

Wild Magic fortells its path; borrowed power returned at last.

…

And the enemies heard it all. And end party. The sparkling lights shine throughout the night and everyone falls asleep in a mossy area. The next day. Fen awakens with a handful of mushrooms in his pocket and a bleary memory. There's also a hyena in front, that seems suspicious. Regardless, he takes the mushrooms to Freld for further inspection. They're a Michinid mushroom, and an unfamiliar substance. But there's a lot of colonies of mushroom and we don't know where it came from. Freld eats the mushroom. And then tells Fen his exact weight - how much he's worth apparently. He pulls up clay statues - 2 mushroom, myconid, people. One male with a red cap and one female with a white sweeping gown. They're part of the network. They are the craftmaster and his lieutenant. The mushrooms are safe and you can hide things from the collective, and you can opt out at any moment. Sprocket is in his tower discusses the network with Freld and makes sure he's okay with being part of the network. Varis wakes up hungover and wanders around the keep to take stock Dallor is asleep in front of Varis' door, and has already taken 3 candlesticks. And already, Dallor has sown an address in his pockets - the address is to a district in Monethern, Market Square, Cauldron Alley. Some pocketchange and some hops. Varis drags Dallor downstairs and finally wakes him. Varis grabs some autumn leaves and thanks Mr. R for sending help. 'you earned it' Then talk to Mac about rebuilding - he's still a little uneasy, but is heading to the training room. It won't be enough to completely rebuild, but it's a start We'll add the town inside of the walls this time. There's also a crater on the beach - an explosion was on the beach. Well then new plan. Build the walls less tall, let's talk to anemone about making the distillery un-teleportable. We can try to figure out something. Of note - leon had a fiend that we couldn't teleport near. The mercenaries seem happy here. Are they staying? Can they stay? Mac'll offer them a position, Devon will answer to him. It also might be a good idea to start looking underneath, and figuring out what's out there. HAL spare time What are we protecting? Magical stuff, the town, the garden, the people, our home. Could we create a diversion, or honey pot? Maybe there could be a training camp towards the right. A fighter's fortress! A mock encampment with a secret tunnel that leads to the real fortress at an advantaged place where it can easily be attacked. To the tower - Sprocket and Brigit Sprocket hears Birt's annoyance, but first he has to find it. And he does!  And then subsequently goes to Freld and lists his door on the market Looking for tenants and hidden people. A window is broken. The whole place has been looked over. The writing desk is still the same however. Looking for magic - one of the tiles is resonating with conjuration and illusion. There's also a divination stone on one of the bookshelves. The top floor has been heavily searched, it's everyone else's information however, not Sprocket's that has been picked over. And there's a diary from The Mole that was also stolen. And it seems like the attic wasn't touched, but the ladder was pulled down and then immediately put back up. It seems like someone stealthed down the ladder, but quickly was put back up. Sprocket picks up the stone - it's a sending stone. He wraps it up, tries to make it a little soundproof. Sprocket begins to deal with the tile, starts to chisel. Brigit and Sally and Cersei walk in, and through dope hand signs, Sprocket tells them to be quiet and Brigit dispels the illusion of the tile. There's a circle underneath in blood and there are more. It's a teleportation circle within the tower. Attempt to deceive the stone - eh? Sally fills us in. She was in the tower for about a week. She didn't see them come in. She was running through the forest in Argos and ran into some elves and some kids and they came here on a boat - a qual'sha'har vessel, The Grounded Swallow. They came in and messed with things, and somehow didn't turn into weremoths. We'll have Sally stay in the forest and keep her from people at night - surrounded by animals. They were waiting for the mole of the ways and the beetle book of some kind. HAL and Daina walk up the mountains to his future fortress There's a grotto with 3 shiny bodies buried under the water. All three heads look up in one moment towards either HAL or Daina. They walk out and bow to Daina, paragon. And notice that HAL has been activated They have the 12 coat of arms, no 13th spear. Last memory - water rolling in, set to wait, vigilant. Location of leylines found, posted, vigilant, waiting

Got placed here, I woke up here. Daina suspects her axe might be here. Daina talks to Chlora. There was a sinkhole, then the mountains. The tomb of the last curator is around. And gives a general overview of the world. Fen heads to the keep and Varis and asks after the fishbowl and candle? But it was Randall. WHOMP WHOMP.

Plans for the inbetweens : Brigit - Talk to birt See if there's any way of getting the prophesy Hang out in the burnt forest for a day. Start a gentle regrowth, become plants for a full day. Talk to cersei and sally, figure out what needs to happen about the temple of life and death Bring back Argent with the locket Setup barbarian camp, walk through champion trial and what it costs to enter/prizes. Warn everything in the forest about Sally's safe zone. Panthers et al will keep watch overnight. Keep watch over the reincarnated for any funny business.

### Key Plot Points
- We had a ritual of reincarnation
- Sally. SALLY?!? Was found in Sprocket's tower
- Varis dealt with the aftermath of the battle, and dealt with the bodies in the Vodram custom. Iris was promoted into the Countess of Claudere Keep (knighted in a way)
- And of big note, the attack happened on the first night of winter.
- More about the reincarnated, and how they live now.
- Evan wants to keep the heart of the faith (stolen from conquis abbey) and the key to the winter's zenith safe from Rimefang, Still need the anchor of pestilence from tumnus in bynar.
- @ note - House of Life and Death was close to the ritual of reincarnation, close to what Brigit saw at the Toad Pool.
- Brigit - talk to Sally, figure out the tower, and then life and death temple
- HAL - fixing up robots, not sure about steps forward.
- Stalwart ordered some beer, but we don't have any! So, let's talk to our new brewmaster - who can't currently taste. But he has a massive book of tastes and stuff to put in beer.

TIME SKIP

Brigit wants to kill him Varis banishes him to give us a minute Fen thinks this is a prank from Birt? Nah. Maybe? For now, we'll capture him, and decide what to do later. There was someone, but they don't know who. A spy? Who was with him? 11 other people. There was a dwarf with a weird beard? Who killed him? Rohas Dresel Brigit gets the barrel of ale from the panther, takes a swig and tells Hugs his answers aren't good enough. Fen wants to know what their buyout price is. His twin brother Lugs, in a pocket mansion, also where Birt died. (personally) What's the STB buyout price? Death. In Dawnpass - Slitzgnard, the head of the STB. Why were they there? They were trying to sell him some coin. Semi-satisfied, the group gives him the ale. Sprocket wants to know what to say to Lugs to get him to trust us He can go eat bugs. Heading back up A kid comes down and warns Varis about something coming over the walls - the weird spider things HALs eyes are shining - he's here for a reason and they're here for the opposite one. Pursuit and Protect kneel down to Commander HAL And we don't really know what to do with these creatures - it's sunset. NOICE A leg melts and lets out a scream, but it's still up. Fen attacks magically at a spider The spiders attack. HAL gets hit. Another spider grabs pursuit, and tries to planeshift, but is counterspelled. Another spider heads up to hit Sprocket, but gets hit by HAL, Sprocket gets hit rough. Fen gets hit Brigit mass cures everyone's wounds and she turns into a Elk, the Shi Shi Gami Daina is stealthed and waits to grapple if someone moves pursuit Sprocket sets up a Mirror Image (thank you Birt), and gets on the broom Varis flies up in the air and casts chain lightning Fail, fail, fail and passes. "so angry it misses" LAIR EFFECTS A fog rolls in from the sea and covers the keep. We can see, but they can't. And, grasping vines rise from the ground and 2 are restrained. Sprocket (+2 images) flies upwards, taking an opportunity attack, hitting the duplicate (+1) Melts the spider into a pile of anachronistic dust. Sleep Hugs joins us, or will if we save his brother. Brigit tries to reincarnate Birt, and it doesn't work. She gets a vision of a cold dark library - but it seems like a hellhole. Of note - the portal is one way. "waiting for the perfect moment to jump through it" Something is coming here, so she's going to dump them in the ocean And also Mlemph is going to hang out and take care of Sprocket's tower And doom-prepper Varis has tons of furs stored that he brings with him. Kasim also found - an amulet of silence, and a mark of a noble And tons of clothes - a shapeshifter…? Down to the cell - Brigit casts Dispel Magic The blubber moves and amber eyes - the shadow of a yanti woman, Minervess. She's keeping tabs on STB and Sprocket and Birt and Fen Fenvis Parnum (someone Fovis/Birt knows) She wants to see the body - we let her see a feather. He's in the shadowfeld. It has a way of directing us where we're supposed to go - fate reigns there, and going against it has dire consequences. We'll allow her to leave, but only if she tells the STB that we need more time to train these troops And she can come back, but has to be honest. If we catch her sneaking around again, she'll be shot on sight. There's a creature loose - someone beyond the stars. Moving on - to the Frozen Forest There's a massive fiery figure in the sky above the Vag'nahar - can't get too close to it - a phoenix! We've taken the crew of the emerald vale with us - including evan and the heart of the faith. We'll send them a message for them to come pick us up. Note - there's wolves, wolf hunters and an old lady. She's at a bend in the river, near the hot springs, just be nice. Hiking through the woods As night falls, there are wolves all around us, but keeping their distance. And we make it to the hot springs - small lanterns are bubbling up from the hot springs Daina's been here before. She created an axe here with her father and when she hit a triangle with her mallet, the lights danced. A vision of her dad appears - something from her memory, carrying the same wry smile, and he welcomes her back. Brigit casts Locate Object, and loops around as a dire wolf. There is an encampment of humans - wolf hunters, but they're distracted and they don't notice Brigit Commune with Nature A powerful wolf in the direction of the town Notable people - dirkin (elderly woman), and a winter fey (rozski) Let's head down to the lady since we know where she is - Granny Dirkin's hall We're not going to hide, but not going to seek her out either. Cool. Going to go to bed. A woman does appear on horseback - Nancy She's cold and lost her pack, we take care of her, Varis heals her She was supposed to assist in the migration to find a way to evacuate monethern - there's a mountain coming here (the titan) We offer her booze, tea and shelter. Find her pack, get her to stay into the morning And sleep Morning Go get pack - some of the animals got ahold of her rations, but it's not so bad. Brigit stocks her up with half of hers. We mend her bags and she gives us a requisition form from the citadel of monethern for 150 gold To the village. The wolf drawn carriage has gotten closer and seems they're getting closer. We hit the main pathway, and Daina feels some melancholy at approaching her hometown. Let's skirt around the village - try to stealth around, so far so good. But, we do come across four elderly dwarves. One of them actually recognizes Daina! They're waiting for this grave to be consecrated. Brigit druidcrafts the rose open - the sign they've been waiting for. It's a grave of a great colosseum warrior, Hill, who fought the great wolverine. They pour the ashes into the flower. Of note - daina fought the wolverine and that's why the zodiac died. They heard a horn - let's follow it. And it was a human? Fovis perhaps? Daina leaves a token at the grave - and gets a vision of a fierce red headed dwarven woman that vanishes as quickly as it appears. Moving forward to the cave We approach the wolf - a group of thirty Brigit casts animal friendship with her, talks to her as a wolf. We mean no harm, we're looking for Daina's axe. It's Fenrir, the zodiac. She see the locket and see's Daina's heart of the mountain We recognize each other and know that we are among kin. Fenrir takes Daina and party to a place with a black rose, she digs and digs and there's the body of Hilm, with an axe. And a spectral wolf releases from the body. She's guarded this moment for centuries, and it's been meant for this moment. Fenrir runs into the locket, but doesn't grant Brigit her form - it's meant for someone else. Daina gains the anchor of war. Touches it and gets another access granted from the automatons. Until next time!

Daina got a vision of her father There was a scout who lost her way and we took care of her, kept her safe, fixed her satchel We ran into dwarves at the flower, opened up the flower with druidcraft, and lots of lore in presentation Headed up to Fenrir. We befriended and spoke with the wolves. THROUGH THE POWER OF FRIENDSHIP we were able to succeed in our quest. Gaining the anchor of war and Brigit gained the wolverine form. We're starting! Daina reads the note, and gently hands it to Sprocket Ethereally sees an hourglass sand above Daina, and it begins falling as soon as he opened the note. 20 minute countdown. Go to Fen, stuck in the cocoon. There's lines from stars that seem far off in the distance. From the frozen forest it's pointing to the serpent and down to monethern. He's falls, and he is stuck in water - up to his ankles. He begins to walk forward. Fen attempts to eat. Varis talks to Fen somehow - figure this out man. Fen travels to the serpent sea, sees an animal, and books it away Back to the party Someone made a bargain to protect the axe with fenrir. She's maghar. She's his greatest legacy. And barnabus helped. BERGS IS BARNABUS. Siblings. Bergs is Daina's brother. Whoever wrote it guarded the anchor. IT'S DAINA'S MOM. Hlin. (Speaks about her father and signs it - Be strong my children.) Daina knows all the info, that she's an orphan, that she's warforged. But she thought her mother died in a carriage, dying from the vagmahar, but her mother was the wolf, guarding. But she would have died well before thorkin's note. Thorkin lied in his note, but it means that he also didn't read her mother's note and kept it sealed. Sprocket informs the party of an hourglass counting down - ~ 9minutes left. Sprocket looks around and notices Fen is changing Varis casts detect evil and good - the grave is consecrated, Fenrir/Hiln. The axe is consecrated. Brigit heads to where sprocket says the hourglass is, and the party comes with us. There's an outline of a human male spirit within HAL's body and Pursuit and Protect. The warforged took a soul, so possibly, this is a spell. The hourglass looks like a scry spell Brigit digs underneath the hourglass as a wolf, there's the remnants of a campfire, and she lights it with Druidcraft. With one hand, HAL has grabbed onto Thorkin's note. Takes the old note and plunges it into the fire. Paradox avoided. Why? - Brigit and Daina Daina has two tattoos now - a spear and an axe. Attuned the anchor of war. Sprocket wonders how much they can sell it for because we have a lawsuit coming our way, and Brigit lets him know we have a magical forest to support the distillery. But we need lawyers…. Daina hands the axe to Brigit, who takes their 'lawyer' and it disappears. Brigit hands it back and it reappears. Also there's a spear in the handle and a hand-axe 'shuriken'. Where next? South but. The plan Brigit transforms everyone into a cloud, including Fen. He is also a cloud in his plane. We transform back and then try to get Fen into electrum. By lining up copper coins, we setup chain lightning and throw Fen turns into a set of electrum coins, making up 190 lbs. 3800 coins! He's petrified. It's a coin with Fen's face and an outline of him with his bow and Mahen Double bagged, and put into the bag of holding. Let's go south! Heading over the city of crownhinge, a large crater in the side of the mountain, there's a green tides of st. ashbury hole on the side of the mountain. Heading over, there's a rolling area of grasslands (as tall as Varis) There are various pockets in the grassland, crop circles of encounters that we're skipping. Huddled masses of humanoids mixed with spiders - relating to the drab in a way. They're huddled in groups. Around the drab, Varis hovers and turns into himself and takes a closer look. They are super desecrated. Brigit also takes a closer look 1) to turn into one later and 2) to see how close they're related to the drab. They're the second stage of the drab. Back down south to conquis abbey. But, stop outside of town at the clearing. There are people coming towards us. One runs at us and tells us to move. (Sprocket with boots and longstrider) On the road there are tons of guards - not one regiment, but a random assortment of soldiers. But we've seriously impressed the guard who suggests that we might want to stay. Into the city The court of the creators. It's packed in here, but everyone in here is drab. They've been called here for something. A massive dragon is in the center of the abbey and Brigit reaches for the heart of the faith. We send out to Lorelei and they can't come, they're dealing with a dragon and Evan is hurt, they need our help. We have no way of getting there. They'll have to be okay until tomorrow. Let's go to tinker town! The phalanx of Daina, Pursuit and Protect and HAL make the way through the unmoving, unending drab. It's eerie inside of tinker town, only hearing loud gears. And there's a weird coating of dust over all the buildings. "Are there any buildings that sell sprockets?" Brigit looks at the footprints and just as she figures out they're deceptive, Sprocket opens the door and an arrow fires at Sprocket, who thinks that this trap was set by a gnome. A bearded dwarf comes out and grumbles about missing him Varis misty steps behind him, Brigit walks up once he's disarmed. Sprocket walks into a makerspace, and comes upon the motherlode of tinkering stuff. Varis gets annoyed and banishes him And in the meantime, Brigit and Varis setup a trap for him to land in so that he gets pulled up into a beautiful lure - upside down and pulled up into the air. WHAT THE. The helmet falls off, drops the crossbow With Sprocket He was taking wire and wrapping it up into a faraday cage. Sprocket successfully convinces the party to keep one of the Fen-coin's as a coin (unaminous minus Fen) because of the realization that the doe can heal him. We throw the lightning! Fen becomes back into his form, but now above his shoulder he's missing a tiny portion of coin. The healing spirit is around Fen and gets him back. Varis heals him with healing spirit and Brigit heals him with goodberries. Fen wakes up, and Sprocket lets Fen know about everything - we turned him into coins and back. He grumbles and then immediately falls back asleep. A knock at the door Varis misty steps outside and turns invisible The guy is looking for squatters at the home of the Hidelson. Nathanial Brinswitch. We successfully deceive the guard into thinking we were looking for the hidelson's as their guests - simply looking for them. Especially considering we knew the kids names! Freenie and Harbrin. The robots come outside as the gust of wind from Varis blows at Nathanial. And he is blow away by Daina and the robots. The dwarf is gone, he shoots at Varis. We head out. Sprocket convinces Varis to give back the crossbow. However the CHAOS CREW is leaving it in another trap. What's next? Can we get to the ship? No. Maybe. If there's a teleport then yes. Varis finds one with his sense. Sprocket feels tiny and doesn't want to slip through, but Brigit offers to carry him as a backpack and he agrees. NPC - man with dark black leather, carrying a skull on fire as an incense burner, with a sword out, plague mask. Ignored There's a Groaning Forge Inn that we're headed into to find the portal Uncertain of who is drab and who isn't. The bartender talks to us about the Emerald Vail and pulls out a mug - Hair of the dog. Brigit insights him and he's genuine. His ship is the hair of the dog. We all take sips and he finishes it, but we need a portal. Another patron cuts off the mug, puts it into the bar and opens up the portal We also sell 100 casks of dwarven brew - through the portal, no drab allowed. THROUGH THE PORTAL Dragons all around, catastrophe everywhere, the ship is overtaken. Evan is running, and losing from Ryefang.

## Chapter 94T -- Death of a dragon'Heart heist
**Author**: Brigit
**Party Track**: Tieflings

### What Happened

TLDR https://docs.google.com/presentation/d/1tiY3gXhl_KfVWRWyYmAYoFEqRnlWcZ9qgmwikpgYt3s/edit Battle time We arrive in the ship, right as the prismatic breath shoots towards us Distract the dragon to get upstairs Varis blesses - Daina, Sprocket and Brigit. + 1d4 Daina hides, invisible and goes to the stairs There's a dragonborn in the stairwell that are strange and don't seem native to mardov Upstairs, yelling - fall back to the upper level. Dragon makes a strength check to see if it can come in, fails, then passes. It moves a little bit further in. Brigit barely saves from the dragon's breath Sprocket and Varis and everyone get hit with a prismatic breath, taking cold damage. Brigit casts Wall of Fire, blocking the dragon's vision of the group and causing damage to it. And then moves up the stairs - lots of dragonborn, very more dragon. Brigit stays pretty hidden, but i s still seen by one of the allies, not the dragonborn. One of the dragonborn jump up and roars out a beam from its mouth, a necrotic ray against the ally. They want the genasi to come with them, to be delivered to volcanis. Daina hears Blaine concede, bring them to the third floor to get the heart. But realizes that he's telling us where the heart of the faith is. Sprocket taps the boots of speed, heads upstairs and finds the dragonborn and Daina, loudly. Varis heads upstairs, fires a cone of conjure barrage at them, all but 2 take full damage. And then hides in plain sight. All the dragonborns turn to see Brigit. Yay. Daina rages, FUCK IT, and attacks. And then sends Pursuit and Protect upstairs to the heart of the faith. And then armor spike attack When the enemy died, it grew postules and then explodes. Enemies turn, coming for Brigit. Doesn't hit Dragon gets hit by the wall of fire, and then withdraws. But then the back wall of this level disintegrates and appears. Bites out with each of its 5 heads 2 to Daina, 3 to Brigit Brigit casts Wall of Fire, cutting off the dragon and then turns into an Air Elemental Dragons again One moves, Daina attacks. Another stabs his wounded brother, CRIT, killing him and then taking his own life, releasing a petrified gas. Another comes toward Varis and attacks, missing. Another attack Brigit, hitting, but she maintains concentration Another sends an energy ray towards Daina A few more attack daina, hitting a few times Sprocket heads downstairs in a mad dash, leaving all of us. Bites his tongue to damage himself, and then goes invisible. Outside, Rimefang is crouched over a very bloody Evan. Rimefang wants to open the temple and rule the world a la Tiamat. Sprocket quietly wants to fly up to the top floor and enter there Oh hey. A heart of the faith, sitting on a dais by the steering wheel. Varis is visible, and misty steps upstairs, but not before hitting two of the creatures down below, clearing the way. Pursuit and Protect head upstairs, making their way to the heart of the faith right after Sprocket. Reaching out to touch it, there's some lightning that stop them from touching it. Daina attacks two of them, hitting and hitting and hacks away at these statues One dragon jumps away from Brigit towards Daina to grapple and takes an attack. And just fails hard at grappling Daina as she back hands him Another dragon multi attacks Daina Big dragon retreats out again, slamming into the top level Brigit whirlwinds, slamming an enemy away and killing him. Mastermind exhales noxious breath But we're immune/resistant to it Another dragon moves to Blaine and Daina attacks him, but he still dies, adding another point to the petrifying Blaine. Sprocket touches the heart and disappears with Dimension door And, Fen begins to stir What now? Sprocket points to a specific person, Kite, that Sprocket has pulled off the heist and he's back by the portal. Sprocket touches the heart to Fen and his eyes glow with a blue light, the same hourglass, on its side. Fen seems to be having a conversation with something/one not there. Fen's hands rigor mortis onto the heart of the faith, softly petting it Kite announces the message. Varis hears the dragon, but is above, points to one of them to let Evan know we've got it. She sends something upwards before they hide and Varis comes back downstairs Shoots at the mastermind Its death hits out at daina with lightning, then hits Brigit, then hits Varis, removing his concentration and everyone's Bless Shoots again, and again CRIT And then teleports away downstairs Daina smashes a dragon, turning into acid, uses her axe at a spear and then get the last dragon, but CRIT misses. Pursuit and Protect bring everyone back down. Brigit moves Blaine behind her, and then attacks the last dragonborn, slamming into him, killing him and spinning around him to protect everyone from his airborn death throes. What happened with Fen? Sinks into quicksand, somewhere seemingly underwater. And then appears on the top of the windmill willow. With a female sphinx, Lady Constantine, Leonidas' sister Mahen is made of shifting purple sand and appears, Constantine owe her a favor - due to her taking over the yellow knoll The have a common friend - the serpent. From her, it's Leonidas' fault that St. Demeter walks again She rescued Fen from the Leviathan and now wants to ask him a quest Seek the herald of winter and St. Demeter Fen is handed another egg, and then receives a shot of adrenaline and motivation to his system - he'll be able to revive Mahen, but has to travel to the winter zenith. There's some power locked away that's needed now. Everything fades to sand and Fen awakens to the scene as we left it. Sprocket fills Fen in, and how HAL carried him across leagues and leagues And on Fen, there are the markings of a monarch butterfly - wings, but they're not fully formed yet. Fen snatches the heart Sprocket - I turned you into money once, I can do it again So the dragons… Everyone comes down from above, we all get ready to go through the portal. Varis and Brigit look outside, Rimefang has been absorbing Evan's essence/power. Evan's down. We're taking heart of faith, bring evan to trade. Booming voice - it doesn’t matter where you are, I'll find you. Flee rats from your sinking ship. Lorelei resigns to losing the ship again. She feels a calling back to Conquis The ship begins to turn over, just as we all teleport away to the sand seas The sand seas Oswald greets us and then Trxie jumped in to say hello, well reamed into by the pre-recorded message. Where to now? Lorelei? Evan came with another, a tiefling, Vera Saterfell - she's a tiefling that smuggled Evan out and helped everyone with a glamor to the magister's circle. Fen wants to take the egg/heart to unlock the winter zenith And to determine if it's an egg or not, he dispels the magic on it. A 9th level spell. Goodbye True Resurrection. Sprocket is able to tell that a REALLYY spell was dispelled on that heart. Sooo either really bad or really good. Sprocket inspects it again - there's something there that he doesn't understand, can't possible fathom what is in the object. Bartender doesn't have a bar, but it's an array of weapons. You grab a weapon and it turns into a drink. He's the only one working. Daina gets a metallic tasting everclear and added to other flavors. Stronghold, or winter zenith? Or what? Sprocket knows the herald of winter - Fovis. An old companion of ours. (Eric's character) Also. Fen is a little stoned/loopy. Hooray! We've made a decision, we're heading to Anemone. Shots first Brigit grabs a blue crystal with a wood handle - a large flagon of steaming blue liquid - goes down smooth, with gin aftertastes, beautiful drink. Brigit savors it Varis grabs a brooch - a thimble of red liquid - fire brandy, not good liquor at all. Zero taste. Fen takes the raw blue crystal - small amount of thick blue liquid (moves like molasses), - sour that fades into sweet that lingers, perhaps indefinitely. Sprocket sips his water Quick short rest as we hang out, drink, enjoy, get rumors Eavallan forest is being pillaged for war measures, by orcs and the like Brigit talks to some orcs from Stra'tranar ruins. Asks if anyone has missing, not deserted, not killed, but missing. Note: war is between tygras logar and dawnpass Logrammar is missing, was mourned. Nothing from bynar and conquis. Sprocket talks to someone, see if anyone has seen nocturnous or fovis. Yep. Varis wanders into an alley to call out to Reynardine. Is he part of it? Yep, seems so. Confirmation that he's safeish, not dead. In autumn where it's on fire and in rebellion and everything. Rimefang knows where we are, but he's okay enough right now that he's not coming after us (completed one of two objections) And warns to stay away from Bynar unless we go armed and dangerous Grackle is locked in the winter zenith Watch out for fen We're all being judged Riddles and taunts The saints - Demeter, constantine, ashbury and brutus. Why are there 4, there should only be 3. i.e. we're on the cusp of the 4th gard'daemon, and that's why demeter has appeared To Magister's Circle i.e. Sprocket's second home To the alabaster seat, Anemone Brigit tries to bear hug anemone, but she's surrounded by a crystal, and instead bear hugs her peryton. Fen holds out the heart of the faith, but Anemone can't touch it - it's potential and power even she doesn't understand She says it's not broken, but the spell on it is gone. It's one of 4 artifacts holding the cloth of this petal together. Gently, she informs us that we've done important things, but not quite in the realm of breaking it. Every time we've left a heart, we've lost it. Maybe we shouldn't leave this powerful artifacts in other hands But first, does Fen have to leave and find Fovis? TBD Additionally, she's fended off many people looking to take the evil robe. Brigit makes two flower crowns for anemone and her peryton Bed time! Varis at the last minute tells Brigit that Logrammar got taken Nice! Wait. SHIT.

HEY BUDDY I just wanted to take the time to let you know from the bottom of my heart, that YOUSE A BITCH.

Love Brigit

Pod opens, Sprocket is revealed.

I expect coffee and breakfast in the morning

Logrammar

I hope you're doing okay. Heard you got taken. Do you need a rescue? Let me know and I'm there, Love Brigit.

I am fine. Things are strange in the autumn feywilde. I am needed here. Lok tala Papers on Sprocket's desk were eschew To be continued next time.

**Author**: Brigit
