### What Happened

TLDR https://docs.google.com/presentation/d/1BHIwsxvXbn0bjXTTqLcQ0c8NfGOpbOK_vv8JqpvnnY8/edit#slide=id.ge39eba7d62_0_62 Nocturnous Wayne Has met many refugees from Argos Perhaps it's time to get a new prophesy to a new direction - to the alabaster seat! Teleports, but according to some issues with the system, Nocturnous is asked to declare his purpose for arriving at the magister's circle And also that Randall sent him a golden ticket for any performances and shows in the bardic colleges And also gives him the FYI that the circle is under new management - with tieflings and orcs and magic oh my Batman meets Anemone What is he fated to do now? “I see green, red, black and blue joining white. Keep your step, gather your allies and avoid the bite. One day you shall overcome Envy’s grasp, only then shall you rejoin the Kai at long last.” Down below, a commotion - someone is attempting to steal a robe and Anemone needs Nocturnous' help. (off screen) chaos and ridiculousness. A day and a half pursuit across the city. Nocturnous is covered in blood from nose to toes, arriving in the bardic college, to meet Randall. He has a choice to make, and he should head back to anemone. Don't come back to the bardic college Uh. There were bulls in the play. And bull tamers. But now, not so much. The cloak has been returned Meanwhile Brigit wakes up with a sticky note on the forehead - go get potions Looking around, Fen has irish goodbye'd Options Goldentooth under siege Hindersnap Finkle and the heart of the wild Cersei and sally and getting back the crown Mandu - long lost brother and STB wanting our shit Let's go kill Finkle! Sprocket grabs some scrolls from his house - tiny hut! Fireball Heading downstairs, we'll walk directly into Nocturnous Wayne, and we only recognize him because just saw him at the Dallor Dallor Bill incident. Character descriptions @ eliner to add in from video Of note: nightmare is different, more envious, and the cloud reacts to varis - showing sun whenever he is near, and Sprocket and Nocturnous are a step towards undead To the shops! The apothecary Potions galore, hanging suspended from the rafter, and when Daina approaches, two collide and the window fogs over. Utter chaos Warder, the shopkeep, approaches. Everything magnetic has been pulled forward Let's get 2 potions for everyone, and then 4 for Daina. And then, send along a shipment to the distillery every 2 weeks And some mage armor potions, and shield of faith potions Another shopkeeper appears and is stoked that we're here - Emily She wants to be an adventurer someday And a special potion - a fishbowl of storm giant strength If we want it cheaper - we'll need storm giant sweat and blood from a plant She wants an adventure - she wants to come with us. We don't want to take her to Argos, but we could have a small shenanigany adventure to the distillery. And then she could get beer and training. And in return, we get the potion of storm giant strength - 6 actually that'll last 10 minutes each. You might get some shards of glass, but you'll be strong enough to chew it. But, we need to make sure her dad doesn't know The Unicorn's Tail A series of multicolored kiosk red (amulet with metals on it - an amulet of the planes - 120,000 gp, provided by Randall as a donation) Orange - porcelain potion of cure moderate wounds [300] SOLD -> Brigit Yellow - garlic water on a plate, magic circle of chaos [750] SOLD -> Daina Green - A kama, scythe [8,300 gp] Blue - Spite, goth, dwarf chain. Sheds light in the darkest of places. It's destructive 18,300 gp. Magnahar weapon, but it looks new violet [wand of hawthorn birch, abnormally large fireball (12 casts) 2475gp], etc, selling only one item SOLD -> Varis White - thin hazel rod, with a serpent carved along it. Not for sale? It's seeking it's wielder. Mathiau of Windhaven.  It allows the wielder to transform into certain animals for an hour - zodiac animals. [19,000gp] Brigit holds the locket above it, it erupts smoke. She transforms into the snake seen, and then the stag and the rod follows. But only one shopkeep - including warder. Brigit writes a note in her diary - and immediately feels a thousand eyes on her, remembering that wizard strongholds can copy non-magical books. Hero's feast under the tree in the center of town Hero's feast! It's going to be a somewhat primitive feast, think very natural with a lot of barbarian flair. I want it to be on the ground by the tree, in the morning dawn light. There's a small campfire in the middle with some smouldering herbs in it to produce an earthy, spiced smoke. Not spicy, but just to make it a little more festive, like rosemary, sage.

There's food strewn about in a rough circle, with really basic, mostly non-processed food of the forest - fruits, mushrooms, nuts, maybe some cheeses and tons of bread and a big wild boar's leg. And some ale and wine. Basically a barbarian's charcuterie board.

Brigit creates a poultice of mud and berries for war paint on her face and hands and arms (like henna) in addition to the meal if anyone else wants some war paint. And I also want some special ceremonial bracelets and anklets that have little bells and bones and dried herbs on them. Short rest Scry for Finkle He passes Brigit sees the body of Breeze/Calil, torn asunder, held up as their being forcibly ripped from their body above a cauldron of unknown contents. Daina and Nocturnous recognize that it's pestilence. There's tar between his hands that he's making into a zenith shape. As he twirls it, Breeze loses more of their hue - their soul Finkle is combining with the heart of the wild, and he notices that I'm looking at him. Nocturnous is reminded of some cleric by the hieroglyphics in the back, Gergal, the scribe of the dead. The remainder hero's feast, we're sending to randall, emily and the interns Let's go! To the teleport We're met by the same half-orc as before. He walks us through the procedure, Brigit is very impatient, ready for war. We're going to close the portal behind us, and then message to get through. Temple time Calil appears, speaking. There's no way it's her, but Brigit checks in. It's not her, and it's not Breeze (who usually speaks), the tile underneath this thing is indented. Noctornous gets a headache from trying to read everything on the walls - all the languages - abyss, rebirth, dragon. Fight Brigit casts Earthbind on not-Calil, which turns into a black icarus model of the zenith, just larger. The loops wrap around, but the monster is able to break free (NAT20 -5) Finkle notices and laughs like crazy Daina dunks in the flask, successfully pulled it out, and drinks part of the storm giant's potion. => 11 min. Daina literally doesn't know her own strength as she steps forward, breaking tile underneath The magic circle lights up, everyone takes necrotic damage Nocturnous attacks with Envy at the zodiac - and then punches again and again Someone is trying to attack our thoughts, and we're fine, but envy begins moving on its own Varis takes shield of faith potion, and then attempts to cross the line, suceeds. Behind the creature is another - an amalgamation of technology. A previous artificer's guild member is taken over. Finkle controls him and has a magic mouth that he can speak out of. And then casts guardian of nature He is also aware that we're in an illusion - the room is bigger, calling out to the rest of us. Sprocket pulls out Blade Barrier spellscroll and casts it The dragon starts its turn, and inhabit's Envy, taking control of it. Brigit casts Greater Restoration on Envy, tearing the dragon from it. Daina rages and attacks, feeling as if she's cutting through oil and tar and pitch. NAT 20! 57 points of damage! Dragon casts telekinesis to grab Daina's axe But fails! Lich starts inside blade barrier, takes damage. And then casts a fireball on everyone. Lotsa damage Nocturnous Wayne hits at the dragon again and again Killing it by pulling apart the pitch-y tar structure. As it disapates, Daina pulls out her soul bag, but isn't able to capture it Exits the circle, getting the finger of death, but still surviving Varis casts hunter's mark, flying up and shooting at the mechanized thing Sprocket can't tell where the spells are coming from, not exactly Dispels the wall of swords, and then create's bigsby's hand. Like a baby bird in the jowls of a golden retriever, the hand envelops Sprocket and takes him out of the circle, safely The circle erupts again, giving us damage Pursuit and Protect move to go kill Finkle HAL also moves through to get out of the circle Brigit downs potion, turns into Fire Elemental, exists circle and gets hit by a ray of frost Daina gets out of the circle, and attacks the gnome. With the bonus action, attacks with an armor spike attack Lich points at Varis and he falls down, dead. [Power Word Kill] To be continued.

TLDR DM lead in:

https://docs.google.com/presentation/d/1nWJhGxxLnPiypSXq0eBNJhR3udLDfl3fGQ_ET6UtGy4/edit Small edit: Nocturnous was wandering around magister's circle with the cloak on. And also, the seller of the Rod of the viper is Mathia Nuum (the one in charge of stealing orphans, orc-war leading to Cloud city falling out of the sky) - also a known yuan-ti. Quelil/Breeze is with Finkle, and some kind of Pestilence is being created out of them (normal pestilence comes out of children's soul, what kind of pestilence comes out of a child of the forest?) Nocturnous can read all the words in this tomb - an illusion of sorts Back to the fight Nocturnous downs a potion, and sets up patient defense. Moves up to the Finkle/not-Finkle figure Varis is uh dead. Sprocket calls out to our last hope - Reynardine, Reynardine, Reynardine. He appears, but not fully revealed, arriving invisible. He's calling on behalf of Varis. But, he's also not supposed to be here - territory does not belong to him, but he has to answer us when we call out. He can help, but it'll cost him. Worse than the backup plan? There's consequences, but he'll come back as is. But it has to be on neutral ground, not here. Do what you will, we have a way out, but we have to fight this guy first. He dissipates, but when he does, the room shifts. We're able to see the room itself now. And in the back corner, Quilil is attached to a pillar. At her feet, there's a caustic hole in the ground (uber pestilence) In parallel, Bigsby's hand grapples the not-Finkle. Success! Sprocket lashes out at the not-Finkle with magic missile Brigit steps into not-Finkle's space, catches it on fire, and then touches it twice to further harm it non-Finkle casts ray of frost but Sprocket counterspell's it. Daina hears chains behind us, but hits at the non-Finkle, hitting it twice solidly, raging, and the mechanical suit begins to break down. Also hit with the armor spike and continues to hack at it. Finkle vows to harness Daina when all of this is over Non-Finkle rips off the suit, throws it off to the side. Then melee spell attacks Nocturnous, but it's not enough Attempts to touch the thing on the ground, Nocturnous dodges away, and non-Finkle plane shifts Reynardine calls out - that was very dangerous. Non-Finkle notices him. Nocturnous bites non-finkle, and the chamber begins shaking. Non-finkle is dead - two marks on the side of his neck Pouring down his mouth is black blood. Envy's gem cracks - nocturnous has made a choice End of combat Back to Reynardine is clapping Brigit goes to Quilil - can she do revivify? She's hurt, can only move half her face, but she can. Bigsby's hand grab Varis Daina comes over, removes the chains, get her out of the weird position Nocturnous doesn't want to make anymore vampire things, maybe? The unconscious mind doesn't agree. Envy won't stab Brigit gives Quilil a diamond and she begins to revivfy Bright light of the raven queen appears Varis is seeing the white, bright light. Knows what the shadowfell looks like. The Raven Queen is before him as he's in a grave 6 ft deep, she extends a hand towards him Varis takes her hand and is back alive! Shaking off a paralysis, and grogginess, but is okay. R's plan would have probably turned him undead. Oof. Glad we had a different option. Sprocket is investigating the mech suit The gnome is in the process of turning Sprocket, with the phylactery, the raven queen's touch, and a lich, an explosion erupts. Ramses calls out, he's back, he's freed, he'll rule again, Gard'daemon is no more. The phylactery is gone, the playing card is burnt. The gnome tells us we have to go The gnome enacts an act of refuge on Reynardine Reynardine snaps, and we're back in the feywilde. In the feywilde Where the elemental planes are now merged with the Feywilde The gnome is Archibald Hollingsworth - Finkle's son Reynardine gives us the run down Finkle was turning into a lich, and with all of the pieces, he became Ramses/Vecna With the heart of the wild, he owns the forest of death and is probably now coming for Brigit who owns the anchor of death, the locket. Reynardine is very glad that Varis is back, un-shambling and all, especially because he gets his knight back. Without Fen, he might actually be able to get us out of the Feywilde without a time delay Daina's hungry. And without further ado, cheeseburgers appear. And Daina can no longer hiccup. Eats at a barbaric pace Any advice? Avoid argos, find the rest of the children. Summary Feywilde is at war, winter is fighting everyone. Harvest zenith is just killing Elemental plane - many evils. Air was quelled, somehow. The oceans are being plagued by a serpent/leviathan. Fire is coming up from the ground, underdark is in rebellion. The pestilence from breeze, we're going to want to retrieve The shadowfell - was bleak and dead, one giant grave. Sprocket still has reasons to go, but we don't have the card anymore. What about legendary curses? He can replace them! Sprocket pulls out his spellbook. Reynardine strides across the room, shrinking down to a normal size, and very carefully looks at the book, but doesn't touch it. You have many curses. Yeah, do you ever feel like a plastic bag, drifting through the wind. Gives Sprocket a choice. Remove this curse, or gain back memory. Someone's been messing with your mind Randall appears - a choice. Reynardine waves Randall away Sprocket wants his memory back MEMORY ALL ALONE IN THE MOONLIGHT Young Sprocket in Hindersnap, with a pocketwatch. Edward Zail/Ned Zallock appears, 'you're the one' Their fates are intertwined. One of the two of us becomes a necromancer to rule the world. He's found the key to the shadowfell. He wants to make a deal, and then if so, they might not see each other again. He wants to become the necromancer, and he'll take Sprocket's curse. He lost someone dear to him, Gideon, and ned will ensure his safety and return in return for some knowledge, and the key to the shadowfell. One more promise - he'll be surrounded by those who care for him very deeply. If Sprocket goes to the shadowfell, ned will follow him. He has no interest in necromancy. Memory wiped. Sprocket traveled across the sea, turned back to magic, and was reassigned to Argos. Joined the STB to do good. And everything happens as it did. Meanwhile, Sprocket has a look of realization. Second memory - final round of the magister's tournament Final test - bear the mantle the longest, one of you must die Anemone wants to refuse, but they can't You can only carry the burden if you understand it leads to death Anemone casts a spell at Sprocket Anemone kills herself with Sprocket's hand and a knife. Sprocket revives Anemone, with necromancy, and Anemone takes the alabaster mantle. A loophole of sorts - she stole Sprockets kill, and thus completed the final round of the tournament, becoming the alabaster seat. Sprocket is the necromancer he was accused of being, and knows how to raise the dead. Possibility of being a triplet soul with Ned Zallock/Edward Zail Sprocket checks his hands - still only 5 fingers. Shadowfell secret - porcelain mask (with a diamond on it) of the raven queen allows you to enter and exit the shadowfell. Had knowledge, gave to ned zallock, forgot and now has again. Quelil - doesn’t feel whole without Breeze. He was separated and turned into a substance she couldn't stand to be near. We have to get him back if we can. Varis - checks on his bond with Reynardine, still good. Checks on the troops - how many have been brought out of the graves. He can only collect them once they're buried in Vodram. There are some rebirthed/revived people from Vodram. He made a deal with Veccna, there are 13 people with crystalline hearts - portions of god's souls. There have been a few lost - tabaxi, friends of ours, Ned. They were all selected based on their zodiac sign.  And also the dead one (ned/ed took sprocket's place). Finkle was always going to be Veccna's body Brigit was given the quest to collect all the  zodiac forms, and in the discussion learns that she's not the stag's zodiac, he just gave the quest. Brigit talks about wanting the rod and expresses that it's cool and she wants it and with a snap of his fingers, Reynardine gets it for her. Just takes away her ability to turn left. 1900gp/a heist, or turning left, Brigit chooses the rod. Daina speaks to Finkle's son/Archibald Machu will take you in if you claim religious refuge. Sun crested over and the zenith opened - chamber for the heart and chamber for the anchor. His mother touched the heart and changed, went crazy - a crown of madness They were stuck in the zenith, eventually Finkle showed up with Machu elves and Finkle grabbed the gem, someone else took their place and Jafar now has the crown of madness. Archibald was dead, his mom was dead, finkle picked both of them up, and shipped them back. He woke up and we were there. He's okay but not okay. Poor kid. He's a thrall, and belongs to Nocturnous. A cylinder around the heart, got the crown of madness Heart vs madness. You control it from the heart, but the power comes from the anchor. Detect magic - enchantment, very powerful. Identify! Holding it, there's a compulsion on Sprocket's mind. Geas spell. A hefty spell. But Brigit didn't have to take a hit, and the locket has all the base forms of the zodiacs. Seems maybe suspicious? Sprocket walks towards the R statue that R was enormous by. The pair walks up the temple and … they both become bigger and bigger as they go further along the temple. Such a size king. It's not an illusion, it's just how things work here. Please keep it between us. And Sprocket knows he could make a deal, but doesn't, and R makes beds appear. Long rest We wake up to Reynardine making breakfast, directing woodland animals with a pan flute. Varis awakens, and the first note on the pan flute is heard. We should be able to get home easier, but first Varis has to talk to someone - whoever gave him Horn. Reynardine was supposed to be the knight, but he points to the moon, the someone who was? The doe has granted him the power, Lily. She'll get us out of the feywilde. He can get us to the moon. It's possible the Raven Queen wouldn't have let us back, she's a bit heartless. There's going to be consequences when we get back - Vecna now walks Mardov. Gard'daemon is complete and we're now in uncharted territory. Knew of 2 gods of death, but with Sprocket's revelation, there's a third. He's close to being able to protect the feywilde, he could protect us if we relocate here. Varis wants to give Mardov a chance, but it was born from a goddess of death, of life and death. Doomed from the beginning and a battleground of gods and chaos. To the moon! For keeping his secret of where he's been, Sprocket gets all of Daina's hiccups Four hippos appear, with wings. We fly up to the moon, which does smell of cheese The doe, lily is fused with the moon, she is the moon. Lily has Fang, and has promised it away already Dying and being brought back is a chance for redemption The one who destroyed Vodram is loose on Mardov. Is there a chance? There's always a chance with mortals Lash out against your creator. She gives Brigit the doe's form, and bestows her gift. Brigit found a shortcut, but it's not enough to get the full power of the locket. Any guidance? Where should we have the final fight? Which zenith? The forest. Topple Jafar. Chop off the head of the hydra before it has a chance to grow back It's a demon of our own making Nocturnous will be our guide Just entering isn't enough because the enemy knows how to do that too The shadowfell - she can grant us masks and access at great personal cost that she has already paid. One mask. Porcelain and diamond. The card will still work to take him, and he can bring one more. To the magister's circle! But there's no sacred tree left there. To Faughart instead. She hopes to never see Varis again, for it would mean they're both gone. We arrive in the middle of a heated discussion of the children of the forest. To be continued.

In Faughart, surrounded by all the reincarnated ones - Dallor, TR, Mia, Wyndell, Quelil, Daina, Freld and Brigit Mia talks about children who have been lost - Cycle, Scale, Bugs, Frost, Haystack. 13 in total, but only 8 left. So the rod would command someone to find the children and protect them. It was Xeres rod. Daina gives Chlora control, since she was the leader Randall is gone, but better stay protected. This group stayed children of the forest, but now they're being hunted. Which child should we go after first? A CHOICE And Randall appears, but it doesn't feel like Blossom is there 1/2 group Breeze, 1/2 abstained Shooting star flies off to Breeze/a way to turn Breeze, and the other choice just got harder. The comet heads directly west Before he leaves, Varis takes a good look at him - Randall appears as celestial-ish? So, the next plan is to follow the comet, find a way to turn Breeze-pestilence into Breeze again. What about the intern? Taking some time, let's check in at home, figure out what's up. We had to save people - thorkin, stewart little, breeze, eveylanndrin, giddeon Vessels? Daina, hal, scamp, nyx with a vessel and sherpa with a vessel And we had questions to be answered (in pptx) First dream - green and gold, everything went to hell. They were all part memory, part recreation of things that have yet to be. A lot of people wanted to upgrade their magic items, so we started to put things in chests. Some people got crystals, some people got sucked in, nocturnous just went his own way Sprocket figured out the key - open one box and put another one inside Mouse hole - nocturnous, found the nagpa. They gave him a choice, he has to slay a queen, but which queen to slay - Raven queen, or others? Casaval had unfinished business with a nobleman from Machu. Second vision, Varis' vision. Ned Zallock was there and Varis had to decide to die. The fight continued, not everyone agreed with Varis dying. Of note, Chlora needed to be in the ritual of rebirth, and breeze. Sprocket was all alone. Well, with Knob and HAL. Ned Zallock was also there. Sprocket decided to fuck with Zallock's day and stab a sword into the spellbook, making it so he couldn't get Mordred's spellbook. At the same time, HAL dropped his mask, gave it to Knob and went back to the other side. Sprocket flew over a waiting army of souls, to a mausoleum where the valley of anchor's is in Mardov. Because Varis had to die. So Varis died. And of key note, he's the way he is because he was the first to be murdered by the first necromancer of vecna. And he's the answer to the curator's plea. So he gets a do-over on a new petal. In Brigit's vision, we needed to do another ritual of rebirth. HAL could be one, but he declined. Also figured out that to ascend the anchor of death, we have to gather all 13 souls Sprocket kept running and went to speak to the Raven Queen. He met the Matron Goddess of life, death and fate within RavenFall, always has been waiting for him. THE LORE GIVEN TO SPROCKET (also in previous notes) Brings back more gods of death. Vecna and the Raven Queen, and its why she allowed all of us to live way back in the beginning. She's been pulling the strings so that we could fulfill her wish to die. She wants to start everything over, complete the final Gard'daemon. Raven Queen - Vecna are twin souls Well actually their triple souls - raven queen/sabine, vecna/mordred, xerses/ramses With the triple souls of Ned Zallock, Sprocket and Ed Zail ? Raven Queen's answer to the question is the zenith's. Sprocket has been tasked, aka must, aka been given a god mission. MUST activate the zenith if we want to make it forward within this cosmic joke. - And the abishai, the real baddies appear. They want the diadem, dragon souls and tiamat to return.

## Chapter 105 -- Anchor of War Silenced'Dwarf Shotgun'Two fireforges in the hand is better than one in the bush

### What Happened

TLDR https://docs.google.com/presentation/d/1s-9Wi8pn9IqTPrfsUE83Hm1MF3wF_de5/edit#slide=id.p3 We stopped Therzadun, kinda. And ascended the axe Hint: no idea what happened to the dwarf king Hint: there was another path through the mindflayer pit, it's drawing the drab and things are getting weird. This is where the mindflayers went Where we were had a circle of 13 dwarf statues, companions of the party who have been picked off through the year(s) Hint: someone still hasn't had all 13 powers, reginald never got to that point We got through the beholder by running, made it to the rest of the party, Daina dived into the lava and ascended the axe. Daina plunged her axe in and turned the volcano into a dormat one. Bergs made the final blow and killed Reginald, unleashing the maul of the Phoenix (his weapon) We stopped the shenanigans of Peraxis with the tadpole and then Bergs and Nocturnous fell down a hole that was previously lava. And we thwarted Therzadun, a signal light leaves and the mountain zenith opens. Flash to the Mardov feywilde, in the dusken wood, where the harvest zenith is There's a battle occurring around this new warrior. Another warrior, silence and a woman, Umbara Skuttle Within the battle are the 4 elementals Joshua Coolbreeze, Champion of Dust, steps forward to fight the air elemental. Josh casts something, vines shoot up from the ground and restrain it to the ground The rest of Josh's companion's jump forward, attacking the air elemental, destroying it. Once the elemental dies, a light shines out, as a sort of signal Sunlight in a springtime glade, aka Springtime Gladebop, is commanding the troops and everyone heads over to him Silence stands over Springtime Gladebop and cuts his throat. Removing his mask, he places it onto the bleeding out tiefling Behind the mask is a writhing, wriggling, mass of bugs, tadpole things GUESS WHO'S HERE? Reynaldine! He knew he would do something, but this is just boring Josh tells the truth, umbara says that he's lying, the blade is nowhere to be seen…. Josh intimidates, gets Reynaldine to back him up, opening up his cloak and disappearing. Umbara invokes the covenant of the stone table. The cloak falls, the leeches form into a small portal and Reynaldine is left in pain. Josh jumps into the portal, smearing blood onto himself to make his way through Into Tartarus As they fall underneath the surface, they fall for ages and end up passing the mountain zenith, hidden underneath the ground Peraxis is unimpressed, so much wasted potential. And he seems annoyed that he's in charge of war, not pestilence, which like yeah, makes sense Bergs wants to welcome Peraxis to the network, but at the moment he says that, he gets a note that Timothy is in the Vag Mahar - he was called by someone else? And alludes to a god having 3 points of failure Nocturnous throws a rope at Bergs, then Nocturnous takes his sword, ties a rope around it and tries to stab the wall NAT 20 Indiana Wayne For a moment, there's a touch of magic on them, Nocturnous knows that it's Levitate, Bergs doesn't. JUST LET IT HAPPEN They are successfully not dead, suspended in the air. A warning - as the anchor of war is silenced, a new paragon is crowned. You fight today to find out if it's a paragon of muriden (god of dwarves) or therzadun. Good luck COOL RUNNINGS Josh has jumped through the portal, where is he headed. He lands in the mines, around the 13 mind flayers. Silence is suspended in the air, Umbara is coughing loudly A voice calls out - what you have done is righteous, traveled to a far destination. Silence holds out his hand, 13 tadpoles fly out and land onto the 13 mind flayers, seep in and then they all grow a porcelain mask. Around Josh is a white light, channeling his divinity, some other power is surrounding him. Another voice comes out of Josh's mouth - Back off, the god voice speaks. The mind flayers leave, silence falls and then walks out the door Tries to get back through the portal with some blood. But before making another action, the portal opens and a massive bear, barrels through. It runs off in a child's voice. Hot on its heels is Fen Darkeyes! He verifies Josh is part of the autumn court and then gets him to follow him. All around, the drab are acting on their own. Josh follows Fen through a wild goose chase through the city Note: fen is the knight of the twisted meadow, spring court, but seems cool enough But, he doesn't feel like spring anymore, and feels like a 4th of each season. Perhaps following a new path They run into a massive two-eyed beholder Back in the stone sanctum Brigit sits down for a rest Sprocket looks into the door - it's the Grand Foyer of the zenith Is there treasure? In the meantime, Sprocket touches a statue The helm, Jakob Hardhammer begins to chat with Sprocket Knowledge about the zenith - the heart, anchor, domain, archangel. This one contains, Bracchial the righteous. If all 4 things are put together, something bad would happen - make a seal perhaps. There's 13 seals and one is within the zenith, Bracchial has it! Jakob wants to tell Gareth that he can eat when he's dead. HAL just walks into the zenith, his eyes aren't focused on anything else. Can't stop him, so we'll catch up in a bit. Sprocket walks back and forth between the statues, delivering their shittalk to everyone Most interesting is Slyvia. Varis and Brigit sit down to rest, eat some jerky and relax while the rest of the party catches up Lily was almost almost consumed into the madness, so she contemplates life and loss And thinks about how she can join the team She tries to heal herself fully, but can't quite get it. Brigit touches Lily and casts Lesser Restoration, it almost goes away, but it'll come back Calls over Varis and Sprocket to come up with a plan. Varis hits with the poison spray and then Brigit hits with Lesser Restoration again. Varis creates a healing spirit, Lily the doe, that comes around and heals up Lily Random information Note: Umbara Skuttle is the evil one who created Timothy, baby mind flayer, trying to learn how to be a human, and a star spawn. And there were eggs from toads. Bergs gossip In Dvinter castle, he reforged the hammer and that's how the phoenix got released and now the phoenix is somehow tied into the hammer too. So, what's next? Bergs, Nocturnous and Peraxis are at the bedrock. The maul casts some light, but they can see the bottom of the zenith The bottom is glowing purple? Peraxis teleported away And Bergs senses Timothy close - right above. The network is getting attacked and Bergs wants to defend it. And it felt like 50% of the drab disconnected…. Subnetted? Partitioned? Directly beneath the zenith, is a manhole. Tiamat and Bahamut are fighting for her and her sisters Possibly one of her sisters was down in the ocean, but she doesn't want to go there. She wants to be on the Emerald Vale Or she wants to be with Mahen, who she knows, maybe? She's got to reform her corporeal form, but for now she's just like this She wants to hang out with zodiac, maybe The gems line up with zodiacs - she wants to be with the stag or the doe or the pangolin or something Brigit flies on over, with Varis, she wants to be reunited with a zodiac, I can't create a zodiac, but maybe we could summon her one tomorrow. The diamond turns into an egg in Varis' hand, and a multifaceted zodiac-progenitor gem in Brigit's who quickly hands it back to Varis before it explodes Anything else? Peraxis' body turns into dust LOOT THE BODY next session The unseen servants of maid-Nocturnous' brooding bring us out some champagne. Brigit asks the Alabaster seat who she is supposed to meet Cue back to old monethern Peraxis needs to cease and desist on what he's doing. The petal of mardov has created protectors HE's talking to Ashbury 3rd gard'daemon, Vecna speaking, Peraxis can continue his research, but he demands modifications Vecna saved the project He will delve deep to uncover creatures that can expand his mind. Sacrifices must be made He discovered a vampiric immunity to therzadune's influence, but there must be more to discover Vecna keeps encouraging him to find the progenitors and their weakness, an abundant resource Post-mountain failure. Vecna rips him a new one. He doesn't care what he thinks he's researching, get to rimefang and do something useful. He is annoyed that Peraxis seems to fail again and again. Therzadune is sealed, received his lich, and Vecna has no more need for him. His potential is wasted, but in death he could rid Vecna of these pretender wizards, and he will grant knowledge to Peraxis Sacrifices must be made, and Sprocket is crying. Brigit needed to meet Peraxis.

- Continues forward and jumps on the dragon

## Chapter 115A -- The day the earth stood still'The Naut-heist
**Author**: Rufus
**Party Track**: Full Party

### What Happened

Intro Mysterious blue light draws us to the water's edge, there is a creature underneath the water Some of us see, some of us get pushed into the water Silran - half-elf bard (aka Scott) Been to hindersnap, from the bard college's, messed up in shadowfell Getting by and unfortunately, pulled beneath the waves with all of us Jarnathan - aarokocra trickster cleric (aka Lillian) Fired and resentful, loss of faith and turned to petty crime, and a new distrust of the government Herdov - Locksidan Rogue (aka Bergs) Smuggler, peanut pusher, sneak and camouflage Ana Huckleberry - halfling druid (Greyson/Em) Woods, moved to the city and turned to crime Jack - small goblin, bard (Ben) Creepy, just likes to watch people, and jumps in Things are pulling everyone under the water, fish, red lit eye'd creatures Rufus Goodhollow - halfling ranger (Liz) Troop leader from Windhaven and Eavallen Very excited to meet everyone and bring us along Tilord Kalaiva - Barbarian Monk (aka Chad) Unarmed, doesn't believe in Big Iron Sea of bodies is suspended around the creature, pulled closer but the liquid around us stops being water and we all succumb Tilord is there for an hour + 2 min But, now we can hold our breath for a lot longer now We all wake up in a mossy cave, surrounded by moss The water appears to move on its own, clearing out the mucus for us so that we can breathe again But it's silent here, completely. There's a voice around We need to gather an artifact and then we can sleep and breathe air We have 3 days or we'll die. But, if we succeed, we'll be rewarded 3 red eyes show, like the monster before We're on a beach, tons of bodies The tide goes behind us, out to the ocean In front of us is an army waiting. GO There's a magic artifact somewhere in the city behind the beach, and it's time to storm the beaches ROLL FOR INITIATIVE Jack notices that the ones that grabbed us aren't storming, some are. There's 150 of us on this quarter of the beach Jack then sees a human pulling a team together, a huddle He grabs the people around to make a team And also uses the distraction to pat people's pockets Silran, no longer has his weapons And then Jack hides in the quartet The orcs run up Tries to hit Ana, but misses Jarnathan decides to fly, and heads over to the ones being attacked. Jarnathan tries to cast a spell, but is no longer breathing air The human yells out, Devin Brighthallow, we need to work together The orc have a weird metal collar and throw a fireball and the largest group More orcs run forward Rufus grabs the scimitar from the orc and then blocks and hunter's mark's him More orcs move up and then go to attack Jarnathan, throwing things at him and at Herdov Another orc throws at Rufus, misses Ana turns into a black bear and bites and claws at the orc and then runs around the orcs They move forward and see a halfling woman by the church Orcs attack again and again One hits Rufus Tie-lor fights the orc and taste the metal of capitalism And kills him! More orcs attack Devin and Jarnathan don't get hit Herdov trumpets and charges forward And also looks down and sees that the beach is littered with weapons And finds a single dagger Orcs rush up to the bear hit it and pop Ana out of their form Tie-lor gets damaged yet again War chief in the back - stomps with a ripple effect. It's time to move A t-rex roar as a group of them appear Silran yells at the orc, viscious mockery The halfling at the church goes inside Jack, is probably no longer hidden. But casts Fireball and many many orcs fall. Definitely merit badge worthy Rufus and Silran get hit by the orcs, and Silran tries to cast silvery barbs Jarnathan heals Tie-lor The wave is coming back The spellcaster, orc creates chains from its helmet and fire at Tie-lor Jack helps tie-lor, cuts his words at the orc But uh, they hit tie-lor Tie-lor dislodges the chains and is safe for now Rufus hits the orc with the stolen scimitar and kills him! And then runs up to his buddy, Ana Ana produced flames and then ran away Tie-lor heals and then runs up to meet Rufus Herdov sprays color water from his trunk and then books it Silran runs to the church, with a misty step and makes it into the church The ocean comes back and covers all of us We swim to the church Some look for weapons, Jack and Rufus don't see what they're looking for Hidna, right patch over eye, orcs Namor, large portly, elderly orc Belinda Swiftfoot, with Silran, she has us pull out our cheek - we have some time, but we're going to turn into those creatures within 3 days Jack leans in, very uncomfortably while Rufus welcomes her to the woodchucks Herdov knows the Swiftfood name, but not Belinda. And there was a story of a halfling that stole an artifact from Uma, underneath Tygras Logath Silran lightly plays a tune on a lute, the song of rest Rufus takes inventory of his pack, they took all valuable stuff, so not much Jarnathan speaks at length with Belinda There's a bowl tomorrow, a tournament, and we can win the item We might die, but we could accept a sponsor and either challenge the king or be a part of Logath's personal guard. We just want to get inside the coliseum - gladiators and sponsors. We're going to need some help Jarnathan gets a magic gun, a glocket. Or we could steal things, a heist perhaps? And Sheila, a new friend, a water elemental could help us be able to breathe So, she needs time and we'll go to sleep But none of us sleep, we all have nightmares, Rufus goes around and gets everyone back to sleep and Jack copies, unfortunately. The coliseum! There are orcs and non-orcs, all speaking common We can breathe now Skill challenge! The item we're looking for is underground Can we find some tunnels or sewers? Ana looks around by the ocean and there are pipes below There are pipes that have a grate over them Tie-lor and Rufus help out, but the grate doesn't remove Herdov reduces the iron grate and it falls into the sea Jack stealths forward looking to make sure we're not surprised 50-60 ft in, there's a sign of a creature that lives here. Ana casts pass without a trace on the group, Jack and Herdov sneak ahead We find a creature - snake and lightning Herdov creates a mirr It's a behir, attack with lightning and constrict around you. They eat dragons and speak draconic They like to sleep where they have eaten Our magic item is pointing to the left Rufus holds hands with Jack, we all walk along together We get back to the beast, it's in the left path but we go to the right Things are a bit more domestic We've come to the servant's quarters, everybody is going to stay hidden and Silran is going to go find a servant to get directions. Silran casts disguise self, a plain orc to go find a servant to figure out where we can go SUCCESS. It's a guard station, so we could do the Wookie? Ana's going to turn into a tiny stirge, get past and we can see what's up Silran, Jarnathan, Herdov and Jack are going to disguise themselves And Rufus and Tie-lor are going to be the "prisoner" Ana successfully flies past the guard station, and lands up in the corner or the ceiling so that nobody can reach you. But, avoiding the spiderweb to not get eaten And the "guards" get through the story good The prisoners appear to be initimidating, successfully To get eaten apparently Slowly leaving the guard station Fly ahead of the party Down into the prisoner quarter Jampacked with people in gladiator outfits, but all in cells Ana turns back Jailbreak, possibly on the way back as a distraction Still in disguise, still heading downwards The cell at the end of the hall is open with a key in it There's an open cell with a stronghold door, Rufus and Herdov stay back warily The stone carved walls depict humans, in a greek and roman style An artifact room, and the sound of scraping claws behind There's a feral cat behind us and Rufus closes the door, like butter, and locks the door behind them Through the other door, is the object, a merit badge, a planetarium, a vault But very very large We have to activate the device in order to get to its contents Anyone who can speak an old language looks at the words on the object 4 specific things must be placed in 4 specific places There's a helm in the center, Silran goes to it, and can see the solution to the puzzle CHARADES Scimitar, seaweed, fireball and orc The objects are in the four locations and money pours out of this thing A small orb glows red, green, blue and white And then with a bang, the gun goes off, the CHEKOV GUN Ana turns into a black bear at the door and there's a moment where everyone wait. And Beatrice appears She lied to us, the magic artifact can't get back to the sleepless If the artifact leaves, and gets back to the ocean, there'll be ocean everywhere. Silran casts charm person And this is a stick up, she's pointing the gun at Herdov Quick woodchucks huddle up. Two options: give the artifact to the god and die, keep it here and guard it and stay. Third way? Beatrice is deeply disturbed, but is leaving something out. We could activate the artifact? But the last time it was activated, the city was sent to the feywilde LET'S GO THE THIRD WAY. Activate the artifact. The planetarium begins spinning around The moons come into alignment and we all enter into the feywilde, and time stopped on Mardov

Begin the stabfest And Narcissa grabs the sword, and hellish rebukes it. It misses Attacks again, hits. And smites Brigit/Lox attacks the scared mirror image, kills her! To the void: I don't know what to do, but this is not my end. Prophecy: this phrase will be uttered again Varis has been here before and understands what's 6 feet under. There is a moment with reviving, and we know that there's a second step to death. A way for the undead to exist There's a time right between death and life and life and death, this moment between, Varis imagines Oblivion to be: rest, like someone cut your marionette strings. (this even goes through the gibberish that Josh is still hearing) Mages running scared, shuts the doors to righteousness and justice The shockwave of the crack, the doors can't shut on the justice. Misses, there's more things to bite and kill. Righteousness dies. Justice hit everyone equally. Righteousness hits a 6th level smite. And has 1 HP. Righteousness smites it's killer, Lox, 8d8: 32. Turns to Sprocket, you are responsible for this. You are accountable for this. Octavious runs away, Lox tries to hit, but it fails. Bergs tries to hit, succeeds. And he goes down. He gets back up. Smite hits Bergs, Bergs goes down Each time he gets back up, there's a portion of him missing. To Sprocket; grabs, and a flash of steel, rolls to attack, himself. Dies ontop of Sprocket and hits Sprocket with everything Octavious had been hit by. Octavious stands back up. Bergs' turn, death save: 1 fail Narcissa ghost, wafts over to Octavious and kill him. Sprocket has gone down and come up As the alabaster seat, he is the conduit of all arcane magic. The fabric of reality and magic is tied to Sprocket. The transition between life and death, is the raven queen's domain. Conquis Abbey == Mecca She's there with Sprocket. Grabs his hand and brings him back to life. Sprocket, you have wonderful friends He first sees a dragonborn boot, and secondly sees purple, that same purple that attacked the last Alabaster Seat. Sprocket's last eye is crying purple tears and dissipating. And is visible to everyone What will Sprocket do? The cloak is flying behind Octavious, the wings are coming out of the cloak Pops up, 3rd eye visible, Tons of pink tears are coming up from the ground, and Bigsby's Hand rises up with the tears Aka. The Gnome Yoinks ™ The magical equivalent of yoinking wings off of a fly The cloak erupts, and Sprocket can sees everyone's bones An infinite loop. Righteousness and justice go up and down again. ### Key Plot Points
- https://docs.google.com/presentation/d/1_N1VMSncZN0Q_yPbJAGC496iIv6h8XjIEeGJAtO6kFQ/edit
- Asmodeus is dead!
- A contract was made between Bergs and Josh; a paladin's oath, where Josh and Bergs are bound to a righteous and just path
- A bit of foreshadowing; ash falling from the sky, in Var'tos (aka our house), meaning somehow the forest zenith has been activated. But, Brigit has her anchor of death. Tbd
- Bergs; I may be the newest god, but I am enough. There is no such thing as a lone wolf (2nd instance)
- Asmodeus was struck down by Josh Coolbreeze, channeling his new oath through Bergs
- What does it mean for a god to die
- And why would the Raven Queen bring all these gods to her petal?
- Death fuels the Raven Queen
- Josh kills Asmodeus, pulls off his left hand
